## 10 Failover & d3Net

> **Setup: Failover & d3Net** (`fo_setup`, text preset): These presets read the failover / d3Net state of ONE machine addressed by name. Fill in the selection selMachine and set it to the machine's resource name exactly as it appears in Designer's d3Net Manager / Machines list (the 'name' field of GET /api/session/status/health - not necessarily the Windows hostname). Every preset here uses the object path Machine:"$(liveupdate:selMachine)"; change the selection variable(s) and all presets re-point at once (duplicate the page and use a second selection variable(s) if you need two machines side by side). Readouts: taken over a failed machine (understudy side), failed (actor side), auto failover disabled, network connected, network status code, active, role, running as, understudy targets, plus identity: machine name, hostname, IP, hardware type, d3Net node id, GUI visible, resource status. There are deliberately NO write presets in this category: role, hostname, director and understudy targets reconfigure the session, and the fail over / restore machine commands are not reachable through a LiveUpdate set: they are the module's 'Failover:' actions over the Session REST API (destructive, so they need 'Allow destructive commands' in the connection settings and two presses). Alarm colours: add the liveUpdateCompare feedback as noted per preset (red = failed / offline / taken over / inactive, amber = auto failover disabled). Rows for the connected machine itself, the acting director, offline / failed / taken-over lists across the whole d3Net, understudy configuration, the failover timeout, network adaptors and by-hostname addressing (selection selHost, shared with 03 Monitoring Remote) were verified live and are included. Only the name of the Director's understudy stays experimental (no understudy in the test project). Values update at the state (500 ms) or static (5000 ms) rate; failover indicators are method calls that the Director may evaluate on every cycle, so do not lower them below the defaults.

| preset id | name | purpose | kind / actions | object path | property path | variable | value type / range | writable | update freq (class → default ms) and rationale | source | status | live (r34.0.3, 2026-09-04) | write |

<details><summary>Notes, verifier corrections and flags</summary>

- `fo_taken_over`:  Meaningful when selMachine names an Understudy; on Actors and the Director it is normally false. The strict === comparisons keep the ERROR / PATH_ERROR strings visible instead of rendering them as YES. getByUID(<machine UID>) is a documented alternative object path (fo-02) if names change during a show.
- `fo_failed`:  Pairs with fo_taken_over on the covering Understudy. Property read (no method call), so change-driven updates are expected.
- `fo_failover_disabled`:  Read-only in the pyi; the session-wide timeout (fo_failover_timeout, experimental) is the related d3NetManager setting.
- `fo_network_connected`:  Preferred over networkStatus (fo_network_status) whose integer enum is undocumented. Semantics versus object.active are undocumented; observe both on the rig.
- `fo_network_status`:  Log the values seen while pulling a network cable to map the enum; until then use fo_network_connected for colour rules.
- `fo_active`:  Exact semantics versus networkConnected are undocumented.
- `fo_role`: corrected textTemplate, previewText, notes. The textTemplate shows only an INFERRED label (Solo=0 ... UnknownRole=6, fo-08 verifierNote: 'Mapping remains inferred ... constants are not unambiguously role-tagged') on a normal-tier / doc-verified button; if the mapping is wrong the operator sees a confidently wrong role with no way to notice. Keep the raw number visible next to the label. Read is doc-verified; the label mapping Solo=0, DedicatedDirector=1, NonDedicatedDirector=2, Actor=3, Understudy=4, MobileEditor=5, UnknownRole=6 is INFERRED from Machine class constants, so the raw enum value is always shown in brackets. The property has a setter but no set action is shipped (dangerous write per the design rules).
- `fo_running_as`: propertyPath object.runningAs.name is not the candidate's literal propertyPath (object.runningAs); accepted because the fo-09 description states 'use object.runningAs.name for a string' and the verifier note confirms chaining .name is documented LiveUpdate behaviour, and the null-resource PATH_ERROR hazard plus the fo_running_as_ref fallback are already documented. Keep liveTestPriority high and test before shipping in the normal tier. Chains the doc-verified Machine.name (fo-13) through the runningAs resource reference, which the LiveUpdate doc documents ('Chaining through resource references') and the candidate itself recommends. TEST FIRST: what runningAs returns when the machine has NOT taken over (self, empty name, or a null resource) - if.name raises, the module marks PATH_ERROR and after 3 strikes unsubscribes/resubscribes in a loop; in that case ship fo_running_as_ref instead.
- `fo_targets`: corrected writable, notes. writable is 'no' but the row subscribes to the raw object.targets property, which has a setter (fo-10 verifierNote: 'targets ... with setter at 24146-24147'); the candidate's 'no' referred to the comprehension result. Must be 'yes' (no action shipped, as now). / propertyPath deviates from the candidate's doc-verified expression ([t.name for t in object.targets]); the substitution is justified by the no-comprehension rule and object.targets is a member cited in the verifier note, but this should be stated in notes as a deliberate deviation so the lead can decide whether the doc-verified status still holds for the raw property (it does per pyi 24144 + machine reference page). / textTemplate uses length(), which is a real Companion 3.x expression function but is not in the design-rule list (toFixed, round, secondsToTimestamp, msToTimestamp, jsonparse, jsonpath, bool); keep it but document the raw-JSON fallback explicitly. DELIBERATE DEVIATION from the fo-10 propertyPath: the candidate's doc-verified form is the comprehension [t.name for t in object.targets]; comprehensions are excluded from the normal tier, so the underlying property object.targets (d3.pyi:24144, List[Machine], machine reference page) is subscribed instead and serialises as a JSON array of {uid,path,type}. The property has a setter (d3.pyi:24146-24147) that must never be driven from a Stream Deck - no action is shipped. textTemplate uses Companion's length() (Companion >= 3.0 expression function, not in the design-rule short list); if unavailable, set textExpression false and text 'Targets\n$(liveupdate:understudyTargets)'.
- `fo_hostname`:  Has a setter; deliberately no set action (dangerous write).
- `fo_ip`:  Member name keeps the pyi spelling IP_address (capitals and underscore).
- `fo_name`:  Shows ERROR when the selection variable(s) does not name an existing Machine (module sets the variable to 'ERROR' on an Unable to subscribe reply). Also object.nodeName (d3.pyi:24081) exists but is not catalogued.
- `fo_type`:  typeName is read-only; the integer twin machineType has a setter and is not catalogued.
- `fo_gui_visible`:  Toggling the GUI is REST only (POST /api/session/status/setguimode); startupGuiMode (writable int, startup-only) is not catalogued.
- `fo_status`:  Resource.status is a method, hence the parentheses. Drop the row if the live test shows an always-empty string; object.isBad / object.isInError / object.descriptionExtended are the documented siblings.
- `fo_local_name`:  Key test for the whole subsystem:D3NetManagerSystem family: the LiveUpdate doc only demonstrates subsystem:MonitoringManager and subsystem:RenderStreamSystem. If it fails, fo_local_name_alt (module global) or getByUID(<uid from fo_d3net_ref>) are the fallbacks. What 'local' means when Companion is connected to a non-Director is untested; connect to the Director.
- `fo_local_name_alt`:  Key test for global-name reachability ('from d3 import *' exposes d3NetManager, state,... if the LiveUpdate namespace matches the /python/execute one). A NameError decides between the two routes for every other experimental row. subsystem:MonitoringManager is used as the anchor because it is the documented subsystem prefix, so only the global is under test.
- `fo_director_name`:  The.name chain is read-only; the underlying director property has a setter (assigns a new Director) and is never written.
- `fo_is_director`:  If resource equality misbehaves through the eval bridge, the candidate's alternative is object.d3NetManager.director.uid == object.d3NetManager.localMachine.uid. Amber when false: Companion should normally be talking to the Director.
- `fo_director_mode`:  The D3Net guide assigns d3net.directorMode = True but the r34.0 pyi and reference page mark it read-only; treated as read-only. Siblings actorMode, dedicatedDirectorMode, nonDedicatedDirectorMode and mobileEditorMode (all read-only bool) are not catalogued.
- `fo_understudy_mode`: propertyPath object.d3NetManager.understudy is a sibling named in the fo-25 description/verifierNote (d3.pyi:10279), not the candidate's own propertyPath; accepted for a read-only experimental row because the member is listed in the candidate JSON. Sibling listed in the fo-25 candidate (d3.pyi:10279, verified read-only bool).
- `fo_solo_mode`: Sibling-derived from fo-25 (soloMode, d3.pyi:10248) rather than the candidate's own propertyPath; accepted, member is listed in the candidate JSON. Sibling listed in the fo-25 candidate (d3.pyi:10248, verified read-only bool).
- `fo_has_valid_director`: Sibling-derived from fo-25 (hasValidDirector, d3.pyi:10217) rather than the candidate's own propertyPath; accepted, member is listed in the candidate JSON. Sibling listed in the fo-25 candidate (d3.pyi:10217, verified read-only bool).
- `fo_local_taken_over`:  Only meaningful when Companion is connected to an Understudy; on the Director it is normally false.
- `fo_local_running_as`:  Combination suggested in the fo-26 candidate. Same null-resource caveat as fo_running_as.
- `fo_machine_count`:  Narrow variant from the fo-27 candidate notes (len(...) is a builtin, no comprehension).
- `fo_offline_machines`:  Narrow variant from the fo-27 candidate notes. The variable holds the JSON string of the list, so the colour rule compares against the literal '[]'.
- `fo_failed_machines`:  Narrow variant from the fo-27 candidate notes.
- `fo_takenover_machines`:  Narrow variant from the fo-27 candidate notes.
- `fo_machines_table`:  Candidate's exact expression. Use jsonpath($(liveupdate:machinesTable), '$[?(@.failed==true)].name') in button expressions to pick fields once the JSON shape is confirmed.
- `fo_actor_names`:  The underlying actors property has a setter (session reconfiguration) and is never written.
- `fo_understudy_names`:  Same pattern as the actors row, as the fo-28 candidate states (d3.pyi:10269). The underlying understudies property has a setter and is never written.
- `fo_understudy_table`:  Candidate expression with the null-resource guard from its own notes applied to runningAs. Any non-zero configStatus before a show is a misconfigured understudy.
- `fo_failover_timeout`:  Writable per the pyi and the only sensible failover knob (e.g. set 0 during a risky moment), but the candidate is inferred so the write is excluded by the tier rule; promote to a setValue/nudge control in 10 Failover once subsystem:D3NetManagerSystem is verified and the units are confirmed.
- `fo_host_taken_over`:  Reuses the selHost selection variable(s) of 03 Monitoring Remote (hostname without the ':d3' suffix). An unknown hostname probably yields a null resource and a PATH_ERROR loop.
- `fo_d3net_ref`:  Once the uid is known, every subsystem:D3NetManagerSystem row can be rewritten as getByUID(<uid>) with 'object.d3NetManager.' replaced by 'object.' - a doc-verified prefix. object.name / object.isEnabled (Subsystem base) confirm which subsystem resolved.
- `fo_locked_to_director`:  Uses the module global state on a documented subsystem anchor. Writable bool in the pyi, but the candidate is inferred and the meaning unconfirmed, so no onOff control is shipped.
- `fo_network_adaptors`:  Added at merge. Doc-verified comprehension: experimental tier by the comprehension rule, status kept.

## 11 Templates

> **Setup: how to use templates** (`tpl_setup`, text preset): Templates are generic buttons with literal placeholders; they do nothing until edited (the module does not subscribe while a path still contains a placeholder such as <OBJECT_PATH>; the readout shows UNSET). After dragging one: (1) open the LiveUpdate Variable feedback and replace <OBJECT_PATH> with a Designer object expression (e.g. track:"Track 1", screen2:"LED Wall", transportManager:default, subsystem:MonitoringManager.findLocalMonitor("fps")) and object.<PROPERTY_PATH> with the Python member path (e.g. object.description, object.lengthInBeats, object.player.tRender); (2) each template carries its own variable name (tplString, tplSetNumber, ...); rename it to something meaningful for that object/property pair (letters, digits, '_', '-' and '.') and change every $(liveupdate:...) in the button text and in the actions to the new name. One variable name must watch exactly one object/property pair: the module refuses a second subscription for a name that is already bound elsewhere and says so in the log, because the Set / Toggle actions find their subscription by variable name; two buttons watching the same path must use the same variable name, and their shared subscription runs at the fastest Update Frequency any of them asks for; (3) keep Update Frequency above 0 (prefilled from the State interval setting, 500 ms). Set / Nudge / Toggle actions do not take paths: they write to the subscription that owns the variable name, which is why each control template carries the matching feedback. Number values are JavaScript expressions evaluated after variable substitution ($(liveupdate:myValue)+1 works; the result must be a finite number); Boolean is a checkbox; JSON is a partial object merged into the property ({"x": 0.0} changes only x of a vector); String is sent verbatim. Object, array and resource values ({uid, path, type}) arrive as JSON strings, so use jsonparse / jsonpath in expressions (see Watch: JSON field x). A readout shows ERROR or PATH_ERROR when the Director rejects the path; fix the path, the module resubscribes on its own. The button texts show such a marker (and OFFLINE or UNSET) as it is rather than formatting it: keep the isNumber(...) / jsonparse(...) === null guard when you edit a text.

<details><summary>Notes, verifier corrections and flags</summary>

- `tpl_watch_string`:  Feedback: liveUpdateVariable {variableName: myValue, objectPath: <OBJECT_PATH>, propertyPath: object.<PROPERTY_PATH>, updateFrequency: presetIntervalState}. Companion substitutes $(liveupdate:myValue) in plain text mode; until the first value arrives the readout says PENDING, and a value the Director sends empty (or None) shows as an empty line.
- `tpl_watch_number`: corrected notes. notes could state that a non-numeric variable value (ERROR / PATH_ERROR / $NA) renders as NaN through toFixed; harmless but worth a sentence for operators. Expression mode, same backtick/\n style as src/presets.ts. Swap toFixed for round, secondsToTimestamp or msToTimestamp depending on the member. The isNumber() guard shows PENDING, OFFLINE, ERROR, PATH_ERROR, UNSET, an empty value or $NA as it is instead of formatting it into NaN or 0; keep it when you swap the function. Optional colour-by-value: add a liveUpdateCompare rule once extension 3 exists (no default rule shipped on the template).
- `tpl_watch_json`: corrected previewText, notes. previewText '0.50' does not match what the button renders: jsonpath returns the raw JSON number 0.5, and the expression engine prints it as '0.5' (the module stringifies objects with JSON.stringify, so no 2-dp formatting occurs). / notes assert Companion evaluates jsonpath with wrap=false; this was not verifiable from the local sources (the companion_api research text only confirms the function exists). Soften to 'a single match is returned as a scalar in Companion 5' or verify in Phase 3. jsonparse turns the module's JSON string into an object; jsonpath with '$.x' picks field x (in Companion 5 a single match is returned as a scalar; verify in Phase 3 that an array is not returned for single matches). Replace '$.x' by '$.path' for a resource or '$[0]' for an array element. Pair with tpl_set_json for partial writes.
- `tpl_set_number`: corrected range, notes. range says the module rejects Infinity: false. processNumberValue (src/actions.ts:60-63) checks only typeof value !== 'number' \|\| isNaN(value); Infinity passes and is sent to the Director. Number value is a JS expression string evaluated with new Function after $(...) substitution; '0' is a literal. NaN and non-number results abort with a warning; Infinity passes the check. Writable only if the operator points the feedback at a settable member; the action is a no-op with a warning while the feedback has no live subscription.
- `tpl_set_string`:  String value passes through parseVariablesInString, so $(custom:...) or $(liveupdate:...) references inside the value are substituted before sending. Replace the example 'new text' with the wanted value.
- `tpl_set_true`:  Boolean value option is a checkbox (true). Companion renders the boolean variable as 'true' / 'false' in plain text. Partner of tpl_set_false; both together are the fallback for tpl_toggle.
- `tpl_set_false`:  Boolean value option is a checkbox (false). Partner of tpl_set_true.
- `tpl_toggle`: corrected notes. Row is conditional on extension 4 (proposed). Acceptable as drafted because fallbackIds are given and the notes say to omit the row when the extension is not built; the generator must implement that omission rather than emitting an action id the module does not define (Companion would show an unknown action). Depends on extension 4 (PRESET_CATALOG_RULES §11); the proposed action takes only variableName and inverts the current subscription value. The generator MUST omit this row entirely while the extension is not built (an unknown actionId would render as a broken action in Companion) and ship tpl_set_true / tpl_set_false instead; do not emulate a toggle with setToDisguiseBoolean because its value option is a plain checkbox, not an expression.
- `tpl_set_json`: corrected previewText. previewText '0.50' should be '0.5' for the same reason as tpl_watch_json (jsonpath returns the raw number). JSON value is parsed with JSON.parse after variable substitution (invalid JSON aborts with an error log). The LiveUpdate API merges partial objects, so {"x": 0.0} leaves y/z untouched. Point the feedback at the whole vector (e.g. object.offset), not at a scalar component.
- `tpl_nudge_up`: corrected notes. rotate_left / rotate_right sets are inert unless the preset sets options.rotaryActions: true (CompanionButtonPresetOptions); the row must record this for the generator. / notes claim the action 'fails with a warning while the variable is undefined': correct (Companion substitutes $NA, new Function throws ReferenceError, caught and logged), but state the mechanism so it is not mistaken for a module guard. Same mechanism as the upstream screenOffsetSetPlus preset: the value expression is substituted then evaluated as JS. Generator sets options.rotaryActions: true so the rotate_left / rotate_right sets are active. While the variable is undefined ($NA) or holds ERROR / PATH_ERROR the evaluated expression throws or yields NaN, which processNumberValue catches and logs as a warning (no write). Medium test priority because it exercises the read-modify-write path once for all nudge rows.
- `tpl_nudge_down`: corrected notes. Same rotaryActions requirement as tpl_nudge_up. Mirror of tpl_nudge_up (upstream screenOffsetSetMinus mechanism); generator sets options.rotaryActions: true so the rotary sets are active.

</details>

## 99 Experimental

> **About experimental presets** (`exp_setup`, text preset): Read-only presets that could not be confirmed on the r34.0.3 test rig: it had no Expression Variables device, DMX screen, timecode source, stage venue, Text layer or second transport, and one row (RS Layer Node Status) needs a running workload instance. They may show ERROR / PATH_ERROR; that is not a module bug. Confirmed rows are promoted to their home category without changing ids or variable names (103 rows were promoted after the 2026-09-04 live run).

<details><summary>Notes, verifier corrections and flags</summary>

- `ev_dev_description`:  Property is doc-verified (liveupdate.txt row); only the prefix is inferred from the lowercase type:name convention (resources guide: path type is the internal class name, lowercase; mididevice:bcf2000 precedent). This single subscription decides whether all other ev_dev_* rows can be promoted. Unknown whether the name token must match description or the lowercase path filename.
- `ev_dev_started`:  Device.started is getter-only (d3.pyi:11738). Do not use device.enabled (documented in the devices guide but absent from the r34.0 stub). object.receiving (11734) is a possible sibling but is not a candidate.
- `ev_dev_count`:  Marginal; valid indices are 0.. count-1.
- `ev_dev_float`:  Candidate is writable in pyi and is the intended 'numeric knob', but inferred writes are excluded: read-only here. If the prefix resolves, this becomes the human-readable alternative to ev_uid_float; the set half still needs the subscript-set live test.
- `ev_dev_string`:  Read-only (inferred write excluded). Function-type variables may hold their expression text here (unconfirmed).
- `ev_dev_name`:  Never expose the setter (renaming breaks expressions).
- `ev_dev_type`:  Read-only; type change is not an operator action.
- `ev_dev_error`: corrected textTemplate, previewText. Fixed: the text showed OK for an empty value, which is also what the readout holds when the Director sends None. It now shows the error text as it is: an empty line means no error text, and the state colour turns red only for a text that is not empty. errorText has no docstring; linking it to the help page's 'error state' message is an assumption. pyi setter exists but is never exposed.
- `ev_dev_expression`: note: method call in property path; candidate exists (d3.pyi:16113, returns str) and is assumed non-mutating - experimental only, as drafted. Zero-argument method, no docstring, no doc example; semantics unknown. Method-call property paths are documented in liveupdate.txt. Assumed non-mutating (returns str); remove if the live test shows side effects.
- `ev_dev_float_by_name`: minor: introduces custom variable d3_evname, not in the lead's variable list - documented in ev_setup/ev_x_setup and openQuestions; lead to accept the name. Comprehension result is a copied value, so it can never be a set target (verifier corrected writable to no). Raises IndexError if the name is absent (feedback shows an error). Introduces selection variable(s) selEvName.
- `ev_dev_value_by_name`: minor: uses d3_evname (see ev_dev_float_by_name). Display-only. Plain text (no toFixed) because the value may be a string.
- `ev_dev_all_dict`: corrected textTemplate. Fixed: the draft built the JSON path with string '+' ('$.' + $(custom:d3_evname)). Companion 5 resolves expressions with stringConcatenation off, so '+' is numeric, the path became NaN and the button always showed $NA; the path is now concat('$.', name). The variable holds a JSON string (dict serialised as a JSON object per liveupdate.txt); jsonpath picks one entry; the path is built with concat('$.', name) because '+' in a Companion expression is numeric ('$.' + name is NaN). Whole-device payload every 500 ms - keep the device small or raise the interval.
- `ev_dev_all_list`:  Button text lists the names in index order; the full JSON is in $(liveupdate:evDevAllList) for the Companion variables page.
- `ev_layer_vars`: note: candidate object path is module:<layer_name>; quoted form module:"..." follows the catalog convention but the help page documents underscores for module: references - already flagged in notes/openQuestions, keep as experimental. module:<layer> is documented as Designer expression syntax but track_layer's verifier rejects it as a LiveUpdate object path (contradiction for the lead to rule on). f.value is a ReflectionValue with undocumented JSON serialisation - may come back opaque/empty. Fails whenever the layer is not under the playhead. Layer names with spaces: help says underscores for module: references; the quoted form is used here per catalog convention - test both. Never call f.setFloat/f.setString from a property path (re-executes on every evaluation).

</details>

## Drafter and verifier notes per dimension

_The notes and the critique below are the Phase 1 review records, kept as written. They describe the drafts, not the shipped module: gaps they name may have been closed since, for example the transport commands, which the module now sends as its Transport: actions (see the HELP)._

### monitoring

**Drafter summary.** Monitoring dimension drafted from 27 supported candidates (19 doc-verified, 8 inferred; mon-renderstream-prefetcher-names excluded as unverified). 40 rows: 21 normal-tier readouts in '02 Monitoring Local' (fps latest/avg10/avg60, GPUProfiler Total + 6 sub-series, CPU Total, GPUMemory, ProcessMemory, the five Grafana-only 'Machine' percent series and the two Grafana-only 'Disk' MB/s series - all flagged liveTestPriority high - plus the global graphs-paused flag), 6 normal-tier rows in '03 Monitoring Remote' (remote fps latest + 10-sample avg via $(custom:d3_host), remote monitor expired flag, resolved remote nodeName, MonitoringManager.remoteNodes() discovery list, Machine.nodeName via $(custom:d3_machine)), and 13 read-only experimental rows (local monitor-name / series-name / units / thresholds / raw min-max / category discovery, remote per-node monitor names, and remote Machine/GPUProfiler/CPU/GPUMemory readouts built on the inferred mon-remote-any pattern). No control rows: nothing in this dimension is a sensible Stream Deck write; the only pyi-writable members (searchString, nodeName, expired, pollInterval) are monitor configuration and were skipped or shipped read-only. Every MetricMonitor value uses the monitoring class (1000 ms), booleans the state class, discovery lists/identity strings the static class; never 0. stateColour rules follow the guide (fps error = 10 below a 60 Hz target, warning = 5 below on the smoothed series) and the Grafana thresholds (GPU 17 ms, Machine 80 %). Text templates in expression mode use the same backtick-plus-\n convention as the module's existing fpsMonitor preset.

**Verifier summary.** Checked all 40 rows and 4 text presets of the monitoring draft against docs/research/phase0-candidates.json (dimension 'monitoring', 28 candidates: 19 doc-verified, 8 inferred, 1 unverified). Traceability: every candidateId exists; every objectPath/propertyPath is the candidate string after allowed substitutions ($(custom:host)->$(custom:d3_host), $(custom:machine)->$(custom:d3_machine)); no invented members; the unverified mon-renderstream-prefetcher-names is correctly excluded and every inferred candidate is shipped read-only in '99 Experimental' with bg rgb(70,70,90), status unverified and no actions. Tier/status is correct on all rows. Rule checks pass: ids unique with monl_/monr_ prefixes, names <= 40 chars, variable names [A-Za-z0-9_] and unique per (objectPath, propertyPath) pair, OSC-mirrored/lead-listed names used (fps, fpsAvg10, gpuTotalMs, cpuTotalMs, gpuMemMb, processMemMb, remoteFps), freqClass follows the class table (monitoring for MetricMonitor values, state for booleans, static for names/lists) with no deviation needing freqRationale, no actions anywhere (so no option-id/value-type issues), text templates use only toFixed/round/jsonparse/jsonpath/ternary and reference the row's own $(liveupdate:var) (module keeps booleans/numbers native, index.ts:630-639, so the ternaries work), previewText, sources and liveTestPriority present on every row. Module facts confirmed: feedback options variableName/objectPath/propertyPath/updateFrequency with useVariables on both path fields, so the $(custom:d3_host) inside monr_node_monitor_names' property path is legal. Findings (all non-blocking, ok=true with corrections): (1) no row carries the style 'size' field required by the style rule - add size 'auto' to every row (put in every corrections object); (2) four experimental remote rows (monr_machine_gpu_time, monr_gpu_total, monr_cpu_total, monr_gpu_mem) build object paths not literally present in any candidate by substituting a documented monitor name/series title into the inferred mon-remote-any pattern; the candidate's own description sanctions this ('any documented local monitor name should also be resolvable per remote node') and they are read-only experimental, so accepted, but the lead should confirm this reading of rule (a)/(c); (3) monl_cpu_units combines the doc-verified CPU object path with the inferred graphDescriptor(False).yUnits property (verifier note is monitor-generic, mon-series-names already uses graphDescriptor on the CPU monitor) - accepted as experimental; (4) duplicate button titles: 'GPU mem' used by monl_gpu_mem (MB) and monl_machine_gpu_mem (%), '<host> CPU' by monr_machine_cpu_time (%) and monr_cpu_total (ms), '<host> GPU' by monr_machine_gpu_time (%) and monr_gpu_total (ms) - corrected titles supplied; (5) the two extra '99 Experimental' text presets (monl_exp_setup, monr_exp_setup) are redundant if the generator synthesises headings from homeCategory - drop them in that case, otherwise fine. Coverage: no doc-verified candidate of operator value was skipped without a sound reason (mon-series-by-index, mon-machine-failover, mon-manager-enabled, mon-manager-searchstring and the configuration members of mon-monitor-meta all have valid skip reasons); missingRows is empty. The verifier's 'missed' genlock members (Machine.allRequiredOutputsGenlocked etc.) have no candidate ids and correctly produced no rows.

<details><summary>Skipped candidates</summary>

- `mon-series-by-index`: Index-based fallback (seriesAverageByIndex(0, 1) on CPU); the index-to-series ordering is unverified and the titled CPU Total row already covers the operator need. Troubleshooting aid only, not a Stream Deck readout.
- `mon-machine-failover`: Duplicate: the candidate itself names the failover dimension as primary owner of Machine:"<name>" / object.hasTakenOverFailedMachine(); shipping it here would create a second pair/variable for the same member (fo_ rows).
- `mon-manager-enabled`: Marginal subsystem flag (verifier: 'marginal value as stated'); connection/subsystem health is covered by 01 Connection and by the readouts themselves erroring.
- `mon-manager-searchstring`: Pure UI configuration (Monitoring Manager search box) with no show value; it is the only write in this dimension and a LiveUpdate set on a Subsystem-rooted (non-Resource) path is undocumented.
- `mon-renderstream-prefetcher-names`: Status unverified / verifierSupported false: 'renderstream' and 'prefetcher' are constants on _BlipValue classes that are not MetricMonitors, and two quoted series labels do not exist on the help page. Excluded by rule; RenderStream health belongs to 09 RenderStream (subsystem:RenderStreamSystem).

</details>

**Open questions:**

- CPU 'Total' unit: the guide calls the subscription 'CPU utilisation', the help page defines the CPU graph in milliseconds, and the Grafana dashboard never subscribes to 'CPU'. monl_cpu_total ships without a unit suffix or colour rule; run monl_cpu_units (graphDescriptor(False).yUnits) and read one live value before labelling.
- 'Machine' (five percent series) and 'Disk' (Read/Write MB/s) are evidenced only by disguise-one's official Grafana dashboard; 'Machine' is absent from the help page's Local graph list and the guide says Disk over LiveUpdate is 'in development (DSOF-20732)'. All seven rows are liveTestPriority high - confirm they resolve on r34.0.3 and whether a disk-utilisation percent series exists.
- Remote monitors: (a) does the Director need MonitoringManager.useMonitorByName(nodeName, monitorName, True) before data flows; (b) hostname case sensitivity; (c) do object.remoteNodes() strings and Machine.nodeName carry the ':d3' suffix (decides whether d3_host stays a bare hostname as the setup text assumes); (d) which monitor names other than 'fps' are published remotely (promote the monr_ experimental rows only after monr_node_monitor_names lists them).
- stateColour thresholds assume a 60 Hz project (fps < 50 error, < 55 warning on averages; GPU >= 17 ms; Machine >= 80 %). The liveUpdateCompare feedback is still a proposal; if it is not implemented the rows ship as plain readouts, and if it is, consider a per-project custom variable (d3_target_fps) instead of hard-coded numbers.
- Frequency: research and the critic recommended 500 ms for fps/GPU/CPU latest readouts and 1000 ms for rolling averages; the lead's class table puts every MetricMonitor value at monitoring = 1000 ms, which this draft follows. If a livelier FPS bug is wanted, lower the monitoring class default rather than mixing classes across rows of one monitor.
- seriesAverage edge cases are undocumented: return value for an unknown series title, for nSamples above the FIFO capacity (fpsAvg60), and for an expired/paused monitor; also what findLocalMonitor returns for an unknown name. Combined with the module's subscribe-storm hazard, the Phase 2 guard (skip paths containing '<...>', back off after errors) should land before remote rows with a wrong d3_host are exposed.
- Companion expression details to confirm on 5.0.4: the backtick template literal with a literal \n escape (copied from the module's existing fpsMonitor preset), the ternary operator used in the boolean rows, and that jsonpath(jsonparse(x), '$.Actual.min') returns a scalar for monl_fps_minmax.
- Cross-dimension variable dedupe: monr_machine_node_name uses Machine:"$(custom:d3_machine)" / object.nodeName (variable machineNodeName); if the failover dimension defines the same pair the lead must keep a single variable name and frequency class for it.
- Verifier-found doc-verified genlock members (Machine.allRequiredOutputsGenlocked(), hasIncomingHouseSyncSignal(), incomingHouseSyncSignal()) live only in this dimension's 'missed' list without candidate ids, so no rows were built; recommend the failover/d3Net dimension adds them to its health page.
- Experimental text presets monl_exp_setup / monr_exp_setup were supplied as '99 Experimental' headings; drop them if the generator already synthesises headings from homeCategory.

### transport

**Drafter summary.** Transport dimension (04 Transport State, prefix tr_) drafted from the 49 verified candidates: 35 normal-tier rows built only from doc-verified candidates (playhead beats, play mode string + state enum, playing/stopped/holding, section-play/loop flags, track loaded, speed, brightness/volume readouts with +/-0.05 nudges and full/zero sets, engaged readout + engage/disengage/toggle, track resource + name, section count, chase + raw TC status, pre-roll, TC source/incoming TC/source status, set list, transport name) and 14 experimental read-only rows (seconds/timecode conversions, free-running, bpm, section index/name/next/elapsed/remaining, TC source name, set-list track names, active layer count, subsystem:GuiSystem and named-transport playhead). OSC mirror ids used: trackname (tr-20), currentSectionName (tr-25), nextSectionName (tr-26), sectionElapsed (tr-29), sectionRemaining (tr-28), volume (tr-16), brightness (tr-15), bpm (tr-23), playMode (tr-05 string; the int enum is playModeState), trackposition (tr-03 seconds), timecodeposition (tr-04). All object paths are transportManager:default except the two experimental alternatives (subsystem:GuiSystem, transportManager:"$(custom:d3_transport)"); no custom variable is required for the normal tier. Every (objectPath, propertyPath) pair has exactly one variable name and one frequency class; control rows share the readout's pair. textTemplate for textExpression=true rows is the exact Companion expression source (backtick template literal containing the two-character escape \n, as src/presets.ts does); textExpression=false rows carry a real newline. Style: readouts rgb(40,40,43)/rgb(220,220,220), writes amber rgb(140,70,0), on/full green rgb(0,100,0), off/zero/disengage rgb(178,34,34), experimental rgb(70,70,90), size auto, no connectionState feedback stacked. 11 candidates skipped (duplicates, configuration, track-length owned by track_layer, undocumented tRunning). Track-length and layer rows deliberately omitted.

**Verifier summary.** Verified all 49 rows (35 normal, 14 experimental) plus tr_setup against docs/research/phase0-candidates.json (transport dimension, 49 candidates) and the module sources (src/actions.ts, src/feedbacks.ts, src/presets.ts, docs/PRESET_CATALOG_RULES.md). Traceability: every candidateId exists; every objectPath/propertyPath is byte-identical to the candidate (the only substitution is tr-49's <name> -> "$(custom:d3_transport)", which is an allowed placeholder substitution); no new members, no comprehension outside the experimental tier. Tier/status: the 24 doc-verified candidates map to normal/doc-verified, the 14 inferred candidates map to experimental/unverified, category '99 Experimental', bg rgb(70,70,90), readout-only; no unverified/reject candidate is used (tr-45 tRunning correctly skipped). Rules: ids unique with tr_ prefix, names <= 40 chars, variable names [A-Za-z0-9_] and unique per pair; shared pairs (object.brightness x5, object.volume x5, object.engaged x4) share name and freqClass 'state'; OSC mirror ids trackname, currentSectionName, nextSectionName, sectionElapsed, sectionRemaining, volume, brightness, bpm, playMode, trackposition, timecodeposition are used; action option ids are exactly variableName/value; Boolean values are JSON booleans; Number values ('$(liveupdate:brightness)+0.05', '1', '0') are valid JS after substitution (module evaluates with new Function); template literals use only existing Companion functions (toFixed, secondsToTimestamp, jsonparse, jsonpath) plus the ternary operator, and each references only its own row's variable; textExpression=true rows use the two-character \n escape exactly like src/presets.ts; previewText, sources and liveTestPriority present on all rows. Frequency classes match the class table; the two deviations (trackname -> state, preRollMatch -> playhead) carry freqRationale. No hard rule violations found; all verdicts ok. Advisory (not failures): (1) tr_track_resource uses 'static' while tr_trackname, the same 'current track' identity, uses 'state' with a track-switch rationale - consider 'state' for consistency; (2) tr_named_playhead_beats introduces custom variable d3_transport, which is not in the PRESET_CATALOG_RULES custom-variable table - the lead engineer should add it or drop the row; (3) the ternary in boolean rows is a Companion operator, not a listed function, and is correctly flagged as an open question with a plain-text fallback. Coverage gap: tr-22 (object.track.lengthInBeats, doc-verified) was skipped as 'owned by track_layer', but track_layer subscribes a named track (track:"$(custom:d3_track)"), not the current track; a current-track-length readout belongs beside tr_playhead_beats (see missingRows). Remaining skips (tr-02 duplicate, tr-18 duplicate, tr-21/36/38/39/46/47 configuration, tr-44 covered by monitoring, tr-45 undocumented) are justified.

**Candidates the verifier thought should be added (lead decision in the merge list above):**

- tr-22 (doc-verified): add normal readout tr_track_length_beats - objectPath transportManager:default, propertyPath object.track.lengthInBeats, variableName currentTrackLengthBeats (NOT trackLengthBeats, which track_layer will use for the different pair track:"$(custom:d3_track)" / object.lengthInBeats), freqClass static, textTemplate `Track len\n${toFixed($(liveupdate:currentTrackLengthBeats), 1)}`, textExpression true, previewText 'Track len\n300.0', bg rgb(40,40,43), sources 'https://developer.disguise.one/api/session/liveupdate ; d3.pyi:48065/48068 (lengthInBeats + setter), d3.pyi:48072 (lengthInSec, read-only)', writable yes but read-only row (setter is destructive - never a set preset), liveTestPriority medium, note: fails while hasTrack is false; the drafter's skip reason ('owned by track_layer') does not hold because track_layer subscribes a named track, not the current one, and this row is the denominator for a playhead / length display next to tr_playhead_beats.

<details><summary>Skipped candidates</summary>

- `tr-02`: object.player.tCurrent duplicates tr-01 (tRender) with undocumented semantic difference; one playhead row only. Keep the path for the live A/B check (open question).
- `tr-18`: object.status is the integer synonym of engaged (tr-17); duplicate.
- `tr-21`: object.track.trigger_note is MIDI/ArtNet trigger configuration, not live state; also has a setter that must never be offered.
- `tr-22`: object.track.lengthInBeats is a track-length member owned by the track_layer dimension (and its setter is destructive).
- `tr-36`: object.timecode.SMPTE_clock_type is configuration, exists only on TimecodeTransportLtc and errors on MTC/system/beat transports; TransportManager.smpteClockType() (not a Phase 0 candidate) would be the generic form.
- `tr-38`: object.play_mode is the timecode-chase CONFIG enum with a different numbering from the live play state; showing it invites confusion with tr-05/tr-06.
- `tr-39`: object.timecode_can_change_track is pure configuration.
- `tr-44`: object.player.deltaTime is a rough per-frame time; frame health is covered by the monitoring dimension's fps / gpuTotalMs rows.
- `tr-45`: object.player.tRunning has no docstring anywhere; researcher and verifier both say do not ship.
- `tr-46`: object.remotetransportmode is session configuration with undocumented enum values.
- `tr-47`: object.enableCustomFps is timeline-FPS override configuration.

</details>

**Open questions:**

- Prefix casing: transportManager:default (plugin guide, module HELP) vs transportmanager:default (help-site expressions). Test tr_transport_name first; if only lower-case resolves, every objectPath in this dimension must be rewritten.
- tRender vs tCurrent: tr-02 was skipped as a duplicate, but the live test should subscribe transportManager:default / object.player.tCurrent alongside tr_playhead_beats and record which one Designer's timeline readout follows and whether tRender lags a frame.
- PlayMode.__str__ literal strings (tr_playmode) are undocumented; capture them live before adding liveUpdateCompare string rules. Also confirm the playModeState numbering (0 Play .. 5 HoldEnd) against the observed values.
- LiveUpdate set on object.brightness / object.volume / object.engaged: setters exist and sets are documented as undoable/persistent, but equivalence to TransportCommand (propagation to actors, undo-stack growth per nudge) is undocumented; REST POST /api/session/transport/{brightness,volume,engaged} is the documented command path. Verify on a director+actor session before shipping the control rows.
- Nudge clamping: rows use the fixed '$(liveupdate:var)+0.05' form with no clamp. The module evaluates the Number value with new Function, so 'Math.min(1, $(liveupdate:brightness)+0.05)' / 'Math.max(0, ...)' would clamp; decide whether the catalog format may use it.
- Companion ternary operator ('cond ? a : b') is used inside template literals for boolean rows (tr_playing, tr_stopped, tr_holding, tr_section_play_mode, tr_loop_section_mode, tr_has_track, tr_engaged, tr_engaged_toggle, tr_free_running). Confirm support on the target Companion (5.0.4); fallback is the plain 'Title\n$(liveupdate:var)' form.
- Null serialisation: object.timecode and object.setList return None on projects without a timecode source / set list. How the module stores null in the variable (JS null vs 'null' string) is untested, so tr_timecode_source and tr_setlist show the raw JSON instead of jsonpath.
- Every object.timecode.* row (tr_tc_incoming, tr_tc_source_status, tr_tc_source_name) errors when no timecode transport is assigned and the module drops the subscription after 3 path errors; the catalog should tell operators to add these only when tr_timecode_source is non-null.
- tr_next_section_name and tr_section_remaining use the researcher's index+1 / tLastBeatInSection forms; the verifier's safer Track.findBeatOfNextSection(...) form (documented end-of-track fallback, d3.pyi:49757) is not a Phase 0 candidate and would need a Phase 0 addendum before it can replace them.
- subsystem:GuiSystem (tr_gui_playhead_beats) and transportManager:"<name>" (tr_named_playhead_beats) are untested object paths; the named form introduces the only custom variable in this dimension (d3_transport).
- The exact 'path' string inside the {uid,path,type} resource serialisation (tr_track_resource, tr_timecode_source, tr_setlist) is undocumented; previewText values are guesses.
- No transport commands (play / stop / play section / next section / go-to) exist over LiveUpdate; they are REST POST /api/session/transport/* only. Operators comparing with the OSC module will expect them - consider an HTTP action in the module rather than faking them.
- Update rates: module HELP subscribes the playhead at 100 ms while the class table sets playhead=250 ms; confirm the connection-settings defaults give a smooth readout on a Stream Deck.
- trackname (tr_trackname) deviates from the 'names = static' rule and uses the state class (500 ms) so track switches show promptly; confirm this is acceptable for the shared-variable-name rule across dimensions (track_layer uses a different object path, so no pair collision).

### track_layer

**Drafter summary.** Track / Section / Layer catalog for Designer r34.0.3: 32 rows in 05 Track & Layer (track identity/length/BPM/section counts, section start/end via d3_section, note-at-beat and beat-to-time via d3_beat, cue count, tc_adjust knob, per-layer name/enabled/live/extents/anchored and key-0 pos.x/pos.y/scale/brightness readouts, plus doc-verbatim index-based probes), 26 rows in 06 Layer Control (enable on/off/toggle, tStart/tLength nudges+sets, key-0 nudges/sets for pos.x/pos.y/scale/brightness incl. brightness black and constant on/off, index-based pos.x nudge) and 20 read-only experimental rows (comprehension lists/tables, section note/length/time composites, cue tags, layer crashed/dormant/fields/keytimes, brightness patched/at-beat, text string, video clip). All object paths use track:"$(custom:d3_track)", track:"$(custom:d3_track)".findLayerByName("$(custom:d3_layer)") or the doc-verbatim track:"$(custom:d3_track)".getLeafLayers()[$(custom:d3_layerindex)]. 49 unique object/property pairs, one variable each. 15 candidates skipped with reasons; unverified/reject and all inferred writes excluded. The build script and the validated draft were working files of the catalog run and are not kept.

**Verifier summary.** Verified all 78 rows + 3 text presets against the 57 track_layer candidates. Traceability is clean: every candidateId exists, every objectPath/propertyPath is the candidate's after the allowed substitutions (track_1 -> track:"$(custom:d3_track)", "Video 1" -> "$(custom:d3_layer)", index 0 -> $(custom:d3_layerindex), <beat> -> $(custom:d3_beat), section index 0 -> $(custom:d3_section)), the findLayerByName object path is combined only with layer/keyframe property paths (rule c, supported by the trk-findLayerByName-object verifier note), and the only member additions (.tStart/.tEnd on sectionInfo, field names "pos.y"/"scale"/"brightness", the None-guard on tagAtBeat, the three sectionList sub-expressions) are each named in the candidate descriptions/notes. Tiers are correct: all 58 normal rows come from doc-verified candidates; all 18 inferred-derived rows are experimental, read-only, unverified, category 99, bg rgb(70,70,90); no row uses mod-live-clip-values (unverified) or any inferred write (render, interpolation, looping, KeyString.s, KeyResource.r, cues, defaultTransition). Ids are unique and well-formed, names <= 29 chars, 49 unique pairs each with exactly one variable name and one freqClass, action option ids are variableName/value only, Boolean values are booleans, Number values are valid JS after substitution, no OSC-mirror id is misused (trackName/trackBpm deliberately differ from the transport-owned trackname/bpm). Required fixes (ok=false): (1) eight templates use `jsonparse(...).length` — member access is not in the lead's allowed function list and depends on Companion's expression version; replace with jsonpath($(liveupdate:var),'$.length') (jsonpath-plus documents `.length`); (2) trk_section_note_x designerVersion must be r33.1+ because it calls sectionInfo; (3) trk_x_setup lacks homeCategory, which the generator needs to place the heading; (4) raise liveTestPriority of trk_layer_name_idx / trk_key_posx_idx to high — they are the only doc-verbatim object path and settle the getLeafLayers() arity conflict that the whole findLayerByName route falls back on. Advisory only (ok=true): the row field `writable` means "catalog ships a set for this pair", not "candidate has a setter" (trk_length_beats, trk_bpm, trk_layer_name, trk_key_posx_beat say no although the pyi setter exists) — document that semantic in the catalog header; trackName differs from the OSC id trackname only by case (Companion variable ids are case-sensitive but operators will confuse them) — consider a clearer name if the transport dimension claims trackname; the dimension introduces three custom variables not in the lead's list (d3_section, d3_beat, d3_layerindex) and an empty one produces a syntactically broken path (subscribe-storm hazard) — the host guard should treat empty custom variables as do-not-subscribe; three buttons are labelled just "Length" (track beats, track time, layer length) — consider "Trk len"/"Layer len"; lay_enable_toggle's action carries only variableName (no value), acceptable for the proposed setToDisguiseToggle extension but the generator must not validate it against the variableName+value rule; the two doc-verified comprehension rows (trk_layer_names_x, trk_key_posx_all_x) sit in 99 Experimental with catalog status doc-verified — consistent with the no-comprehension rule, but the lead should confirm that an experimental row may carry status doc-verified. Coverage: no doc-verified candidate is skipped without a valid reason (all 15 skip reasons hold); two optional additions listed in missingRows.

**Candidates the verifier thought should be added (lead decision in the merge list above):**

- Optional trk_sec_to_beat (readout, normal): objectPath track:"$(custom:d3_track)", propertyPath object.timeToBeat($(custom:d3_sec)), variableName secToBeat, freqClass static, candidateIds [trk-beatToTime] (member timeToBeat is cited in that candidate's source d3.pyi:48296 and description). Inverse of trk_beat_to_sec; needs one more custom variable (d3_sec), so include only if the lead accepts the extra variable.
- Optional trk_key_opacity + lay_opacity_plus/minus/full (readout + nudges, normal): propertyPath object.findSequence("opacity").sequence.key(0).v on the findLayerByName object path, variableName keyOpacity, freqClass state, candidateIds [key-posx-v, trk-findLayerByName-object]. 'opacity' is named in the key-posx-v template list and in the dimension openQuestions (expressions guide) exactly like 'scale'/'pos.y', which the draft already ships; the drafter flagged it in openQuestions but did not add rows. Same key-0 caveats as brightness.

<details><summary>Skipped candidates</summary>

- `trk-layers`: Structural list of resource refs {uid,path,type}; layers are unnamed resources so path is empty. Covered by trk_nlayers and the experimental layer-names list.
- `trk-getLeafLayers-typed`: Structural list of video-layer resource refs (read-only per doc); no operator value on a button beyond the count, and len() is not a documented member.
- `trk-sectionToBeat`: Duplicate of sectionInfo(i).tStart, which is used instead because sectionInfo degrades gracefully ("or the end of the track if no such section exists") on a bad d3_section.
- `trk-trigger_note`: Pure configuration (MIDI/ArtNet trigger id); writing it is dangerous and reading it has no show-time value.
- `trk-defaultTransition`: Inferred configuration object (transition mode enum undocumented); no show-time value.
- `trk-cues-raw`: Inferred; TimeSequence[Cue] has no documented JSON serialisation. Superseded by the experimental cue table.
- `trk-layerByName-object`: Duplicate of findLayerByName (untyped Any helper; candidate itself says prefer findLayerByName). Keep as a manual fallback if findLayerByName fails live.
- `lay-render`: Inferred, no docstring, semantics of render vs renderEnable unknown; verifier says do not ship untested. Write excluded by rule; a read-only row has no defined meaning.
- `lay-track-container`: Structural back-reference (owning track resource ref); the operator already knows the track from d3_track.
- `lay-moduleType`: Inferred; Type has no documented JSON serialisation.
- `key-interpolation`: Inferred keyframe configuration (interpolation mode); not an operator control, write excluded by rule.
- `key-looping`: Inferred sequence configuration (loop on/off); not an operator control, write excluded by rule.
- `grp-layers`: Needs a GroupLayer object path; findLayerByName returns a leaf Layer and no group-layer path is documented in the candidates.
- `mod-live-clip-values`: Candidate status unverified (module: prefix undocumented as a LiveUpdate object path); excluded by rule.
- `lay-description`: Do-not-ship warning candidate: Resource.description on an unnamed layer yields the class name; use object.name (trk_layer_name).

</details>

**Open questions:**

- New custom variable d3_beat (a float track beat) is introduced by this dimension for noteAtBeat / beatToTime / tagAtBeat / beatToSection / evalString; confirm the name or fold it into the lead's variable list. An EMPTY d3_beat / d3_section / d3_layerindex yields a syntactically broken path (e.g. object.sectionInfo()) and triggers the subscribe-storm hazard; the host-side guard for unreplaced placeholders should also treat empty custom variables as "do not subscribe".
- Variable naming vs OSC mirrors: trackName / trackBpm are used here instead of trackname / bpm because this dimension addresses a NAMED track (track:"$(custom:d3_track)") while the OSC ids describe the current playing track, which the transport dimension is expected to own. If the transport dimension does not claim trackname/bpm, rename these two rows to the mirrors. Note trackName vs trackname differ only by case.
- Placement of the tc_adjust knob: it is a track-level write (doc-verified setter) but 06 is named "Layer Control"; the three tc_adjust control rows are in 05 with trk_ ids. Move them if 06 should hold every write. Units of tc_adjust are not stated (assumed seconds, step 0.1).
- lay_bright_const_on/off (FieldSequence.disableSequencing, doc-verified setter) were added beyond the lead's explicit 06 list because the guide's constant-brightness recipe needs them next to the key-0 knob; drop if unwanted.
- findLayerByName("...") as an object-path segment is pyi-backed on a documented prefix and documented chaining style, but never shown verbatim. Live test order: trk_layer_name (probe) first; if it fails, the index-based rows (getLeafLayers()[i], doc-verbatim but with a pyi arity conflict) and the experimental comprehension fallback are the alternatives. Missing/duplicate layer names are undocumented.
- Keyframe steps are guesses: pos.x/pos.y step 1 (doc example sets 42.5), scale step 0.1 (reset 1), brightness step 0.1 with an assumed 0..1 range although the guide example sets 25.0. Adjust after the first live session. Field names beyond pos.x / pos.y / scale (LiveUpdate page) and brightness / text (guides) are undocumented; run trk_layer_fields_x once per project. "opacity" is guide-named too and could get the same nudge rows.
- Boolean sets on object.enabled and object.findSequence("brightness").disableSequencing are not shown verbatim on the LiveUpdate page (only key .v is); the setters exist in pyi and the doc says sets are undoable/persistent. Verify live before shipping the 06 category as normal tier.
- Ternary expressions (cond ? a : b) are used in several textTemplates for boolean readouts; Companion 5 expressions support them, but if the lead wants function-only templates they can be replaced with bool()/concat forms.
- The experimental rows trk_layer_names_x and trk_key_posx_all_x are doc-verified comprehensions kept in 99 Experimental only because of the no-comprehension rule; their catalog status is left doc-verified so the generator can label them accordingly.
- nKeys with or without parentheses (LiveUpdate page vs pyi/guide) is unresolved; the normal rows use nKeys(), the doc-verbatim experimental comprehension uses nKeys.

### stage

**Drafter summary.** Stage / Screen dimension (Designer r34.0.3) drafted from the 55 verified candidates: 96 rows (82 normal-tier in '07 Stage & Screen', 14 read-only experimental rows in '99 Experimental' with homeCategory '07 Stage & Screen') over 54 unique (objectPath, propertyPath) pairs, plus two text presets (stg_setup, stg_experimental_setup). 45 candidates are used, 10 are skipped with reasons (visualiser-only or programming-time configuration, inferred colourshift, the whole-stage comprehension). Normal tier covers the Surface (screen2:"$(custom:d3_screen)"): identity/health readouts (name, mesh, resolution, isInError, locked, isInActiveStage(), isConnected, needsContent(0)), geometry (offset JSON readout + jsonSet reset, offset.x/y/z readouts + 0.1 m nudges with rotary support, rotation, scale, one-subscription dict-literal geometry), output controls (master_fade readout/nudge 0.05/set 1.0/set 0.0, holdOutput readout/ON/OFF/toggle with onOff fallback, renderLayer readout/ON stage/OFF stage, alpha and blendMode readouts), tracking (isReceivingFromTrackingSource, hasTrackingSource, trackingSource.description, externalUpdate readout/ON/OFF); the projector (projector:"$(custom:d3_projector)"): name, isInError, hasFeedAssigned(), isActiveOutputModeNone, offset/rotation readouts (no nudges: calibration risk), fieldOfView, master_fade and holdOutput families (Display members combined with the documented projector: path per stage-007/035 notes), render_mode readout + 4 setValue presets, simulatedShutterOpen readout/OPEN/CLOSE, autoSoftEdge readout/ON/OFF (Number 1/0), feather readout/nudge 0.05; and a rename-proof getByUID($(custom:d3_screenuid)) family (name, master_fade x5, holdOutput x4) that is the normal-tier route to LED/DMX screens. Experimental read-only rows: ledscreen:"$(custom:d3_ledscreen)" (name, master_fade, holdOutput, offset), dmxscreen:"$(custom:d3_dmxscreen)" (name, status__, universe, fixtureType.description, isTableMalformed()), getByUID($(custom:d3_stageuid)) Stage (nDisplays(), autoSoftEdge, venue.description) and the two doc-verified list-comprehension readouts (screen projectors, projector screens) which the normal-tier no-comprehension rule pushes down. All inferred writes are excluded. Variable names are lowerCamelCase and unique per pair; frequency classes: playhead (250 ms) for screen offset/rotation (tracking-driven), state (500 ms) for booleans/enums/fade/hold, static (5000 ms) for names/lists/resolution/FOV. Styling follows the fixed palette (readout rgb(40,40,43)/rgb(220,220,220), writes rgb(140,70,0), on/enable rgb(0,100,0), off/hold rgb(178,34,34), experimental rgb(70,70,90)); no connectionState feedback is stacked. Text conventions: expression templates (textExpression true) use a Companion template literal with the \n escape sequence inside it, exactly as src/presets.ts does today; plain templates carry a real newline; vector JSON values are formatted with toFixed(jsonpath(jsonparse(var), '$.x'), n); enums are decoded with nested ternaries. stateColour rules are for the proposed liveUpdateCompare feedback only. Two custom variables beyond the lead list are introduced for this dimension (d3_screenuid, and d3_ledscreen / d3_dmxscreen / d3_stageuid for the experimental rows); the setup text presets tell the operator to store UIDs with the 0x prefix.

**Verifier summary.** Verified all 96 rows + 2 text presets of the stage draft against the 55 candidates in phase0-candidates.json (dimension 'stage'), the module source (src/index.ts stores number/string/boolean values as-is and objects as JSON strings, so the `$(liveupdate:x) ? .. : ..` and jsonparse templates are sound; setToDisguiseNumber evaluates the substituted string as JS; setToDisguiseJSON only JSON.parses, no arithmetic) and docs/PRESET_CATALOG_RULES.md. Results: 87 rows pass cleanly. Hard failures (8 rows): the six object.offset.y / object.offset.z rows (stg_screen_offset_y, _y_up, _y_down, stg_screen_offset_z, _z_up, _z_down) use a property path that does not exist in the candidate JSON (stage-003 lists only object.offset.x; rule 1 'no new members' / 'do not invent members'); they must be removed or sent back to Phase 0 as new candidates. The two X nudges (stg_screen_offset_x_up/_down) write to a pair whose candidate is writable=unknown (verifier: no doc shows a LiveUpdate set on a scalar sub-path); they are not an 'inferred write' by status, so they may stay in the catalog, but must be gated on a live test with the documented fallback (partial JSON set on object.offset) spelled out. Minor corrections: stg_screen_resolution writable field contradicts the candidate (yes); stg_experimental_setup lacks homeCategory; stg_setup text references the Y/Z nudges that must go. Mechanical checks all pass: ids unique with stg_ prefix, names <= 40 chars, variable names [A-Za-z0-9_] with exactly one name per (objectPath, propertyPath) pair and identical freqClass across shared pairs (screenOffset, screenOffsetX, screenMasterFade, screenHoldOutput, screenRenderLayer, screenExternalUpdate, projectorMasterFade, projectorHoldOutput, projectorRenderMode, projectorShutterOpen, projectorDynBlend, projectorFeather, uidMasterFade, uidHoldOutput), action option ids exactly variableName/value, Boolean values are JSON booleans, Number values valid JS ('1', '0', '$(liveupdate:v)+0.1'), JSON value valid, every textTemplate references its own variable and uses only toFixed/jsonpath/jsonparse/ternaries, previewText, sources and liveTestPriority present everywhere. Tier rules hold: every experimental row is read-only, category 99 Experimental, bg rgb(70,70,90), status unverified, homeCategory set; no row is built from unverified/reject candidates (there are none in this dimension); all inferred writes excluded. Cross-object combinations (projector: + Display/Resource members; getByUID + master_fade/holdOutput/description) are supported by candidate notes (stage-007 'exists on Screen2, LedScreen, Projector and DmxScreen alike', stage-035 verifier 'Projector(SlcAwareDisplay) inherits', stage-055 'works for every type'). Lead decisions to record (not row failures): (1) stage-028/stage-044 are doc-verified comprehension candidates that the 'no comprehensions in normal tier' rule pushes into 99 Experimental with status unverified - the tier/status table did not foresee doc-verified comprehensions; (2) the draft introduces custom variables d3_screenuid (normal tier) and d3_ledscreen/d3_dmxscreen/d3_stageuid (experimental) beyond the lead list; (3) projector simulatedShutterOpen and Object.externalUpdate writes are doc-verified members with undocumented runtime effect - keep liveTestPriority high and withhold from the shipped set until tested, as the candidate verifier notes demand. Skip list is complete and justified (all 55 candidates accounted for). Coverage gaps worth adding are listed in missingRows.

**Candidates the verifier thought should be added (lead decision in the merge list above):**

- stg_uid_render_layer + stg_uid_on_stage + stg_uid_off_stage: object.renderLayer readout and setToDisguiseNumber 1 / 0 on getByUID($(custom:d3_screenuid)) (stage-009 + stage-055, both doc-verified; stage-055 notes that the UID path is the recommended route for LedScreen/DmxScreen). The UID family is the only normal-tier way to reach an LED wall, and taking an LED/DMX screen off stage is as show-critical as master fade; the draft covers only master_fade and holdOutput on the UID path. Variable name screenUidRenderLayer-style: uidRenderLayer, freqClass state.
- stg_uid_in_error: object.isInError on getByUID($(custom:d3_screenuid)) (stage-021 + stage-055) so LED/DMX displays get the same resource-health flag the Surface has (variable uidInError, state class, stateColour == true red).
- stg_proj_active_stage: object.isInActiveStage() on projector:"$(custom:d3_projector)" (stage-020 + stage-034; Projector(SlcAwareDisplay) inherits Object per stage-035 verifier) - a projector left in an inactive stage silently outputs nothing; variable projectorInActiveStage, state class.
- stg_screen_fade_half (optional): setToDisguiseNumber 0.5 on screen2 object.master_fade (stage-007 notes explicitly suggest Fade 0 / 0.5 / 1 buttons); useful as a camera-compensation preset alongside 1.0 / 0.0. Same pair/variable screenMasterFade.

<details><summary>Skipped candidates</summary>

- `stage-012`: object.content (which mesh side shows content) is a visualiser-only programming-time setting; not a live-show control.
- `stage-019`: object.parent.description is stage-hierarchy configuration and errors when the surface is unparented; no operator value.
- `stage-025`: object.colourshift is inferred and serialises as a resource reference {uid,path,type}; JSON shape for reads/sets is undocumented.
- `stage-026`: object.projectorViewBlending is a programming-time projector-view compositing option, not an operator control.
- `stage-027`: object.screenBackground is a programming-time appearance option, not an operator control.
- `stage-031`: LedScreen.luminanceNits is inferred (prefix) and visualiser-only; it does not dim the physical screen.
- `stage-032`: LedScreen.viewingAngle is inferred (prefix) and visualiser-only (Lux simulation).
- `stage-041`: Projector.lumens affects the visualiser only; real dimming is master_fade (stg_proj_fade rows).
- `stage-048`: DmxScreen.luminance is inferred (prefix) and visualiser-only.
- `stage-052`: Whole-stage list-of-dicts comprehension on an inferred Stage path; Companion can only show it as a long JSON string, better suited to a custom widget than a button.

</details>

**Open questions:**

- Scalar writes on object.offset.x/.y/.z (the 0.1 m nudge rows, writable = unknown in stage-003) are undocumented; the documented write is a partial JSON set on object.offset, which the plain setToDisguiseJSON action cannot combine with arithmetic (parseVariablesInString substitutes variables only). Shipped v1.0.2 presets already use the scalar path. Confirm on a live Director before shipping; if it fails, the nudge rows must become 'set X to <custom variable>' jsonSet rows or need a module-side numeric-nudge-into-JSON extension.
- The .y and .z offset rows are derived from stage-003, whose source sentence documents '.offset.x, .y or .z'; only .x appears verbatim in the candidate JSON. Confirm the lead accepts this derivation, otherwise keep only the X rows.
- Custom variables beyond the lead list are introduced: d3_screenuid (normal tier, getByUID($(custom:d3_screenuid))) and d3_ledscreen / d3_dmxscreen / d3_stageuid (experimental). The getByUID rows expect the operator to store the value exactly as 'Copy UID' produces it (0x-prefixed hex); confirm whether getByUID also accepts a decimal UID, and whether the Stage editor offers Copy UID at all (stage-051 open question).
- Doc-verified list-comprehension candidates (stage-028 screen projectors, stage-044 projector screens) were placed as read-only experimental rows because the normal tier forbids comprehensions; confirm that this is preferred over skipping them.
- master_fade nudges send unclamped values (e.g. 1.05); whether Designer clamps 0..1 is unverified. The Number action evaluates JS, so 'Math.min(1, $(liveupdate:screenMasterFade)+0.05)' would clamp if the lead allows a deviation from the fixed nudge expression form.
- Projector simulatedShutterOpen, render_mode and Object.externalUpdate writes are doc-verified members with undocumented runtime effect (real feed output vs visualiser, behaviour while a tracking source is attached); all such control rows are flagged liveTestPriority high and should be withheld from the shipped set until tested.
- Display.alpha range (0..1 per pyi vs 0..255 per help text) is unresolved; alpha ships as readout only.
- Chained resource paths (object.trackingSource.description, object.venue.description, object.fixtureType.description) fail to evaluate when the reference is None, which triggers the module's 3-strike PATH_ERROR unsubscribe and the subscribe-storm hazard from INVENTORY.md; either a module guard or the operator instruction in the notes is required.
- Expression text templates use the '\n' escape sequence inside a Companion template literal (matching src/presets.ts); plain templates contain a real newline. The generator must preserve both forms, and the vector readouts rely on jsonpath(jsonparse(var), '$.x') returning a scalar in Companion 5.0.4 - verify in the button preview.
- Feather nudge step 0.05 is a guess (range/units undocumented); adjust after a live test.
- stateColour rules target the proposed liveUpdateCompare boolean feedback; the generator must drop them if that feedback is not implemented in Phase 2.

### exprvars

**Drafter summary.** Category 08 Expression Variables (ev_). Normal tier: 5 read-only rows on the doc-verified object path getByUID($(custom:d3_evuid)) reading variables[$(custom:d3_evindex)].defaultFloat / .defaultString / .name / .type (4 unique pairs: evFloat, evString, evName, evType) plus one labelled name+value readout that stacks two of those feedbacks. No set action is attached anywhere: the pyi setter exists, but a LiveUpdate set through a list subscript is undocumented (only .sequence.key(0).v is shown), so knobs wait for the live test. Experimental tier (99 Experimental, home 08, read-only, bg rgb(70,70,90)): 13 rows on the inferred prefix expressionvariablesdevice:"$(custom:d3_evdevice)" (description smoke test, started, count, by-index float/string/name/type/errorText/makeExpression(), by-name comprehension reads via a new $(custom:d3_evname), dict and detailed-list snapshots) plus 1 row on module:"$(custom:d3_layer)" for timeline ExpressionVariables layers. 8 knob-* candidates skipped as owned by transport / track_layer / stage; write halves of the by-index members skipped (inferred or untested). Two text presets: ev_setup (08) and ev_x_setup (99 heading for the experimental rows). Frequency classes: values state 500 ms, names/types/lists static 5000 ms, health state 500 ms, layer variables playhead 250 ms.

**Verifier summary.** Checked all 19 rows + 2 text presets against the 23 exprvars candidates. Traceability is clean: every candidateId exists, every objectPath/propertyPath equals the candidate's after placeholder substitution and quoting, no invented members. Tiering is correct: the 5 normal rows all sit on the doc-verified getByUID(...) object path; the 14 experimental rows come only from inferred candidates, are read-only, in '99 Experimental' with bg rgb(70,70,90) and status unverified; nothing is built from unverified/reject candidates (there are none in this dimension) and no row carries a set action. Ids are unique with the ev_ prefix, names <= 40 chars, variable names valid, the one shared pair (getByUID / ...defaultFloat -> evFloat) has the same name and freqClass in both rows, previewText/liveTestPriority/sources are present everywhere, textTemplates use only supported Companion functions (toFixed, bool, jsonparse, jsonpath, ternary, template literals). One row fails: ev_uid_knob is a duplicate readout of ev_uid_float (same pair, same variable) whose text depends on a second feedback (evName) that the row schema cannot express, and its id says 'knob' while controlKind is readout - convert it to an explicit multi-feedback labelled readout (extraFeedbacks) or drop it. Three normal rows (ev_uid_string/name/type) rest on rule (c) combinations: doc-verified getByUID object path + defaultString/name/type members that are pyi-confirmed in inferred candidates whose only inferred element is the device prefix; accepted by parity with ev-device-by-uid's own defaultFloat chain, but the lead should confirm that parity counts as 'verifier note supports it'. Minor advisories (ok:true, prefixed 'minor:'): ev_x_setup may duplicate the generator's auto heading in 99 Experimental; ev_dev_all_dict builds the JSON path with '+' instead of a template literal; the by-name rows introduce d3_evname, which is not in the lead's custom-variable list. Coverage: the category currently has zero controls although ev-device-by-uid is doc-verified and writable=yes - the nudge pair is listed in missingRows for the lead to rule on (the drafter's deferral cites the verifier's 'set through subscript untested' caveat, which is defensible); a getByUID + object.description smoke-test row is also missing; the 7 doc-verified knob-* candidates are skipped as owned by transport/track_layer/stage, which is acceptable only if those drafts actually emit them.

**Candidates the verifier thought should be added (lead decision in the merge list above):**

Lead ruling needed - as drafted the whole 08 category has no control at all.

**Open questions:**

- Future set-by-index knob (ev_uid_float -> nudge, ev_uid_string -> setValue) needs, after the live test: (1) getByUID($(custom:d3_evuid)) resolves the ExpressionVariablesDevice and variables[i].name reads back; (2) a LiveUpdate set on object.container.variables[$(custom:d3_evindex)].defaultFloat actually writes (pyi setter d3.pyi:16052 exists, but the only documented set is through the nested method chain .sequence.key(0).v, never through a list subscript on a _BlipValue); (3) defaultFloat is the LIVE value for device variables - the device editor's value column changes and dependent layer expressions re-evaluate (evidence is one useful-strings snippet; pyi UserName is 'Default'); (4) a step size - the variable's MetaField range/step is not reachable through LiveUpdate (MetaField.get is static), so the nudge step must be a preset option or a custom variable, with clamping done in the Companion expression. If subscript-set fails, fallbacks are setting the whole object.container.variables list with partial merge (list merge semantics unknown) or a session/python action outside LiveUpdate.
- The by-name comprehension rows introduce a custom variable not in the lead's list: $(custom:d3_evname) (variable name, case-sensitive). Confirm the name or rename consistently in ev_setup / ev_x_setup.
- getByUID($(custom:d3_evuid)) combined with object.description (liveupdate.txt row) or object.started would be doc-verified-by-combination smoke tests for the UID path, but were not emitted because the lead's normal-tier list names only defaultFloat/defaultString/name/type. Consider adding ev_uid_description as the first live-test subscription; ev_uid_name currently plays that role.

ReflectionValue (f.value) JSON serialisation is undocumented; layer references only work while the layer is under the playhead; quoted vs underscore layer names untested for module:.
