### renderstream

**Drafter summary.** 09 RenderStream: 18 normal-tier read-only rows (17 on subsystem:RenderStreamSystem covering the four documented expressions, per-instance flags/health/machine name, activeLatency(), isEnabled, getLicensingState() and the getWorkloadLayers() discovery map, plus Machine:"..".hasTakenOverFailedMachine()) and 21 experimental read-only rows (11 comprehension summaries on the subsystem, 10 rows on the inferred track/layer route track:"..".getLeafLayers(RenderStreamModule)[n].moduleConfig...). No control rows: every RenderStream member in the verified set is read-only or a transient struct, the only writable member (framerateFraction) is inferred so its write is excluded, and workload start/stop/restart is REST-only. Operators must create d3_workload (Python int workload id from the Cluster Workload widget 'Copy UID' or REST layerstatus workload.uid; prefix 0x if hex), d3_instance (zero-based) and d3_machine; the experimental layer route adds d3_track and a new d3_rslayer index variable. 3 candidates skipped (rs-metric-monitor unverified, rs-machine-status-by-uid ambiguous uid, rs-texture-param-receivers marginal).

**Verifier summary.** Verified all 39 rows + rs_setup against the 42 renderstream candidates of docs/research/phase0-candidates.json. Traceability: every candidateId exists; every objectPath/propertyPath equals the candidate after the allowed substitutions ($(custom:d3_workload), $(custom:d3_instance), quoted "$(custom:d3_machine)", quoted "$(custom:d3_track)", $(custom:d3_rslayer) for <n>); no members invented; no comprehension in a normal-tier row. Tiers: all 18 normal rows come from doc-verified candidates (status doc-verified, bg rgb(40,40,43)); all 21 experimental rows come from inferred candidates (tier experimental, status unverified, category '99 Experimental', homeCategory '09 RenderStream', bg rgb(70,70,90), actions []); nothing built from rs-metric-monitor (unverified) or from the inferred framerateFraction write. Rules: ids unique and rs_-prefixed, names <= 31 chars, variable names [A-Za-z0-9_] <= 29 chars and unique per pair (no shared pairs inside the dimension), freqClass values sensible with rationale where they deviate, no OSC ids applicable, previewText/liveTestPriority/sources present on every row, textTemplates use only functions confirmed to exist in @companion-app/expressions (toFixed, jsonparse, jsonpath, bool) and reference the row's own $(liveupdate:<variableName>). Three defects found: (1) rs_inst_machine embeds $(custom:d3_instance) in button text; Companion's preset loader rewrites every $(label:var) except local/internal to the connection label (report-companion_api.md:124, REPLACE_VARIABLE_REGEX), so it would become $(liveupdate:d3_instance) and render empty - drop the custom variable from the text. (2) rs_exp_dropped_total uses fg rgb(220,70,90) instead of the style fg rgb(220,220,220). (3) rs_machine_takeover's pair Machine:"$(custom:d3_machine)" / object.hasTakenOverFailedMachine() is also candidate fo-01 (failover) and mon-machine-failover (monitoring); all three catalogs must carry one variable name and one freqClass (machineHasTakenOver, state) - the failover dimension (10 Failover & d3Net) should be the owning row and this one should be flagged as a cross-category duplicate for the merge step. Advisories (no correction needed): rs_exp_layer_framerate carries writable 'yes' on a read-only experimental row (correct per the inferred-write rule, but the generator must not derive actions from that flag); rs_licensing's stateColour uses '!=' which the proposed liveUpdateCompare feedback may not support - already listed in openQuestions; sources copy the researcher's pyi line numbers, several of which the Phase 0 verifier corrected by 1-8 lines (e.g. totalDroppedPackets is 47713 not 47706, tLastErrorMessage 47699 not 47692, workloadId 40305 not 40313) - consider appending the corrected numbers in notes; d3_rslayer is a new custom variable outside the lead's list and is flagged in openQuestions; the whole layer route depends on a class-name argument in the Designer object part that docs never demonstrate (report-expr_syntax.md (b)). Skips are justified: rs-metric-monitor is unverified (excluded by rule), rs-machine-status-by-uid is ambiguous about which uid it takes and has no object-path prefix, rs-texture-param-receivers has an undocumented JSON shape and is diagnostic-only. No doc-verified candidate an operator would want is missing.

<details><summary>Skipped candidates</summary>

- `rs-metric-monitor`: Status unverified (verifierSupported false): RenderStreamMetricMonitor is a _BlipValue, not a MetricMonitor, and no doc or pyi ties findLocalMonitor("renderstream") to a graph or names any series title. Excluded by rule.
- `rs-machine-status-by-uid`: Inferred, but the verifier could not establish whether the uid from GET /api/session/renderstream/pools is the RenderStreamMachine or the underlying Machine (statusString does not exist on Machine, so the row would error); there is no documented RenderStreamMachine object-path prefix and it would need a new custom variable for the uid. Outside the experimental scope set by the lead (comprehension summaries and the layer route). The same statusString is reachable through the experimental layer route row rs_exp_layer_inst_machine.
- `rs-texture-param-receivers`: Doc-verified but marginal diagnostic: returns a list of Subscription objects whose JSON shape is undocumented (pyi body not inspected), r33.1+ only, and not something a show operator reads on a Stream Deck. The workload/stream receive statuses already cover stream health.

</details>

**Open questions:**

- Workload ID format: the pyi types it as Python int; the LiveUpdate doc says to copy it from the Cluster Workload widget ('Copy UID') or the deprecated V1 /api/v1/renderstream/workloads, and the session swagger calls layerstatus workload.uid a uint64-as-string. Confirm whether the copied value is decimal or hex (hex needs a 0x prefix in d3_workload) and whether it equals RenderStreamModuleConfigBase.workloadId (rs_exp_layer_workload_id).
- Subscribe storm on stopped workloads: every per-instance row (getWorkloadInstance(...)) presumably returns an {error} when the workload is stopped or the index is out of range (undocumented). Per INVENTORY.md section 5 the module then retries about 10 times per second on any valuesChanged. Until that module bug is fixed, operators should remove or disable RenderStream instance presets while a workload is down; the generator may want a warning in rs_setup.
- JSON serialisation of _BlipValue structs (WorkloadInstance, WorkloadHealth, SubscribedStreamStatus, the getWorkloadLayers() map) is only described as 'recursive' in the doc. All jsonpath text expressions assume pyi property names appear verbatim as JSON keys and that receiver serialises as {uid,path,type}; verify on a live system and also check whether uint64 workload ids / stream handles arrive as JSON numbers (precision loss above 2^53 in Companion) or strings.
- Enum meanings are undocumented for WorkloadInstance.status, WorkloadHealth.status and .priority, and getLicensingState() (only RS_AUTH_MODE_FULL = 2 named). rs_inst_status, rs_inst_health_status and rs_licensing ship as raw codes; capture values live before adding colour rules. rs_licensing uses a '!=' stateColour operator - confirm the proposed liveUpdateCompare feedback supports '!=' (and '==' on booleans) or drop that rule.
- Variable-name reconciliation with 10 Failover & d3Net: machine-has-taken-over is listed in both dimensions. The pair Machine:"$(custom:d3_machine)" / object.hasTakenOverFailedMachine() must carry one variable name; this draft uses machineHasTakenOver with state class.
- New custom variable d3_rslayer (zero-based index into track:"..".getLeafLayers(RenderStreamModule)) was introduced for the experimental layer route because the lead's variable list has no index for it; rename if the lead prefers another id. The verifier also notes that a class-name argument inside the object expression is not demonstrated in docs, so the whole layer route (10 rows) may need the no-argument form track:"..".getLeafLayers()[n].
- Whether comprehension subscriptions that call .health() inside a list (rs_exp_health_list) push change notifications - the doc says health is computed and must be subscribed per instance.
- Units of RenderStreamSystem.activeLatency() and SubscribedStreamStatus.activeLatency (frames vs seconds) are undocumented, so no latency threshold colour is shipped; likewise the min receive fps threshold is show-dependent and left to the operator.
- Companion expression assumptions to confirm in the generator's live test: jsonpath() with a single match returns the scalar (wrap:false) while multiple matches return an array that template-literal interpolation renders as 'a,b'; nested ternaries parse; bool() accepts the module's native boolean values. While a variable is undefined / 'ERROR' / 'PATH_ERROR', the jsonparse-based texts render 'undefined' rather than an error string. Answered for Companion 5.0.4: a marker made them show null, 0 or NaN; the texts now fall back to the raw value when jsonparse() returns null, and scripts/gen-presets.mjs checks every text for it.
- Control gap: workload start/stop/restart/sync and failover are REST-only (POST /api/session/renderstream/startlayers\|stoplayers\|restartlayers\|synclayers\|failover); RenderStreamModuleConfig.startWorkload()/stopWorkload() exist in the pyi but would fire on subscription if placed in a property path, so no control rows are proposed. Missed writable layer knobs (RenderStreamModuleBase.mode, sceneName, animationSpeed) were not in the candidate set and would need Phase 0 verification before catalog rows.

### failover

**Drafter summary.** 10 Failover & d3Net: 17 normal-tier readouts on Machine:"$(custom:d3_machine)" built from the 20 doc-verified Machine candidates (fo-01, fo-03..fo-16, fo-20; fo-02 duplicate, fo-18 comprehension and fo-19 config skipped). No write presets: the only writable doc-verified members (role, hostname) reconfigure the session and failover commands are REST-only. 31 read-only experimental rows (99 Experimental) cover the local machine / director / whole-d3Net views via subsystem:D3NetManagerSystem and the module global d3NetManager (fo-17, fo-21..fo-31, fo-34, fo-35, fo-42), and the session / project / version / licence identity rows via subsystem:SessionSystem and the globals state / ReleaseVersion (fo-36, fo-38..fo-41) with homeCategory 01 Connection and conn_ ids. Inferred writes (fo-31 timeout, fo-32 normal_preset, fo-42 lockedToDirector) are read-only or excluded. No genlock/sync member is a candidate in this dimension (they only appear in the verifier's 'missed' list), so none is catalogued.

**Verifier summary.** Verified all 48 rows and 2 text presets of the failover draft against docs/research/phase0-candidates.json (dimension 'failover', 43 candidates: fo-01..fo-20 doc-verified, fo-21..fo-43 inferred, no unverified/reject). Traceability: every candidateId exists; all 17 normal rows use Machine:"$(custom:d3_machine)" with the candidate's exact propertyPath (only placeholder substitution and quoting), except fo_targets (raw object.targets instead of the doc-verified comprehension - justified by the no-comprehension rule and the member is in the verifier note, but writable must be 'yes' because the property has a setter) and fo_running_as (object.runningAs.name - supported by the fo-09 description and verifier note 'chaining .name is documented'). All 31 experimental rows are read-only, category '99 Experimental', bg rgb(70,70,90), status unverified, built only from inferred candidates or their explicitly listed siblings (understudy/soloMode/hasValidDirector from fo-25, versionName() from fo-38, narrow variants and guards from fo-27/fo-17/fo-29 notes); '<any resolvable object>' placeholders were substituted with the documented subsystem:MonitoringManager anchor, which is correct. No set actions anywhere, so the action-option checks are vacuous; no inferred write is shipped. Preset ids unique and correctly prefixed, names <= 40 chars, variable names unique per (objectPath, propertyPath) pair with consistent freqClass, previewText/liveTestPriority/sources present on every row, freq classes sensible with rationale where deviating. Defects found: (1) conn_setup text preset collides with the mandatory '<prefix>_setup' id the lead will create for 01 Connection - drop it and hand its content to the lead; (2) fo_targets writable flag wrong ('no' -> 'yes') and its textTemplate relies on length(), which exists in Companion 3.x but is not in the design-rule function list - keep with a documented raw-JSON fallback; (3) fo_role renders an INFERRED role-name mapping as the only value on a doc-verified button - show the raw number alongside the label; (4) fo_health_worst adds a -1 sentinel not present in the candidate (harmless, documented, accepted). Generator convention check (not a row defect): textExpression-true templates are written as bare 'Title\n${...}' and must be wrapped in backticks (template literal) by the generator or they are not valid Companion expressions. Coverage: no doc-verified candidate an operator needs was skipped without reason; fo-18 (network adaptors) is a doc-verified comprehension that falls between the tier rules - listed as an optional experimental row for the lead to decide.

**Candidates the verifier thought should be added (lead decision in the merge list above):**

- fo-18 (doc-verified) Machine:"$(custom:d3_machine)" / [{"name": a.adaptorName, "ip": a.ipAddressStr, "up": a.isUp, "rx": a.receiveLinkSpeed, "tx": a.transmitLinkSpeed} for a in object.getNetworkAdaptors()] - NIC link state per machine is an operator-relevant health readout (a downed 10G link before a show). The drafter's reason (comprehension forbidden in the normal tier, doc-verified so cannot be demoted) is a genuine gap in the tier rules rather than an oversight; lead to decide whether a doc-verified comprehension may ship as a read-only experimental row (suggested id fo_network_adaptors, variableName networkAdaptors, freqClass static, r33.1+ note, unknown remote-machine behaviour).
- Optional: Machine:"$(custom:d3_machine)" / object.isBad and object.isInError (Resource base bools, cited in the fo-20 verifierNote at d3.pyi:40459/40483 and machine reference) would give a plain boolean health colour without the undocumented status() string; they are only siblings in the fo-20 text, so include only if the lead accepts sibling-derived members in the normal tier (otherwise leave for Phase 0 follow-up together with the genlock members in the 'missed' list).

<details><summary>Skipped candidates</summary>

- `fo-02`: Duplicate of fo-01 addressed by getByUID(<machine UID>); the catalog addresses machines through $(custom:d3_machine) and no machine-UID custom variable is defined. The UID route is mentioned in the fo_setup text and the fo_taken_over / fo_running_as_ref notes as a manual alternative.
- `fo-18`: Comprehension-based (normal tier forbids comprehensions) yet doc-verified, so it cannot be demoted to experimental; also r33.1+ only, the bare getNetworkAdaptors() list is of non-Resource netsys_NetworkAdaptor objects whose serialisation is unknown, and behaviour on a remote Machine is untested.
- `fo-19`: d3ProjectsFolder is a pure configuration path with no operator value on a Stream Deck; the project path itself is REST-only.
- `fo-32`: normal_preset is pure failover configuration (a preset name); its write is excluded by the inferred-write rule and the read alone is not operator-facing.
- `fo-33`: directorIsX0() is a hardware fact, version-dependent (X1 variants before r33.1) and not show-relevant.
- `fo-37`: getIsNodeInSession(<nodeId>) needs a literal node id for which no custom variable is defined, and the comprehension variant needs the module global d3NetManager on top of subsystem:SessionSystem; session membership is already covered by fo_active / fo_network_connected / fo_offline_machines.
- `fo-43`: state.enableBlackout is a cross-dimension pointer handed to the transport dimension by the researcher; not a failover member.

</details>

**Open questions:**

- Genlock / sync: the task asked for doc-verified genlock/sync members on Machine, but none exist as candidates in the failover JSON - Machine.allRequiredOutputsGenlocked(), hasIncomingHouseSyncSignal(), syncStatus(iSlot), vfcPTPLockState(iSlot) etc. only appear in the verifier's 'missed' list. They need a Phase 0 follow-up (candidate ids + verification) before rows can be added; until then the only genlock signal is the Genlock category (2) inside fo_health_states.
- subsystem:D3NetManagerSystem and subsystem:SessionSystem resolution is the single biggest unknown (the doc only shows subsystem:MonitoringManager / RenderStreamSystem). Test order: fo_local_name, conn_session_name, fo_d3net_ref. If they fail, rewrite the family as getByUID(<uid from fo_d3net_ref via another route>) or use the global route (fo_local_name_alt pattern: 'object.d3NetManager.' -> 'd3NetManager.').
- Global names in the LiveUpdate eval namespace (d3NetManager, state) and bare type names (ReleaseVersion): one NameError test each with fo_local_name_alt, conn_project_name and conn_designer_version settles it for fo_health_worst, fo_health_states, fo_locked_to_director, conn_licence_days and conn_in_show as well.
- Machine.runningAs on a machine that has NOT taken over: self, empty-name resource, or null? If .name raises, fo_running_as (normal tier) must be replaced by fo_running_as_ref, and fo_local_running_as / fo_director_understudy / fo_understudy_table inherit the same risk. The module's 3-strike PATH_ERROR unsubscribe followed by immediate re-subscribe makes a raising path a subscribe storm.
- Machine.role enum mapping (Solo=0 ... UnknownRole=6) used for the fo_role labels is inferred from class constants; read one Director and one Actor to confirm before shipping the labels. networkStatus enum stays raw until mapped empirically.
- d3_machine must be the Machine resource name (status REST 'name'), not the hostname; fo_name is the check. fo_host_taken_over reuses d3_host from 03 Monitoring Remote (bare hostname, no ':d3' suffix) - confirm both dimensions agree on that convention.
- Companion expression features assumed in text templates: strict === comparisons on typed boolean/number variable values, length() on arrays (fo_targets), jsonparse().uid (fo_d3net_ref). Confirm on the target Companion version; fall back to raw variable text if any is missing.
- Failover COMMANDS (fail / restore a machine, apply default routing) are not reachable through LiveUpdate set; D3State.cmdMachineFailed/cmdMachineRestored are methods and must never be put in a subscription (they would execute every evaluation). A REST action (POST /api/session/failover/failovermachine\|restoremachine) is needed in a later module phase for an operator failover button.
- Method-call and comprehension rows (hasTakenOverFailedMachine(), status(), all d3NetManager list rows) may be re-evaluated per cycle rather than change-driven; the state / monitoring classes are set with that in mind, but the actual Director cost of the whole category subscribed at once should be measured, and the comprehension rows raised to 2000 ms if needed (updateFrequency is not part of the module's change detection, so frequency edits need a resubscribe).
- Variable-name collisions with other dimensions: networkConnected, sessionName, projectName, designerVersion, machineName are generic; the lead should check that no other dimension emits the same names for a different (objectPath, propertyPath) pair.
- conn_setup and the conn_* rows are drafted here because no separate connection dimension exists in the candidate JSON; the lead owns 01 Connection and may merge or rename them.
- Unreplaced custom variables (empty d3_machine -> Machine:"") trigger the module's subscribe-storm hazard; the generator or module needs the planned guard (skip subscribe while the path contains an empty or placeholder name).
- Inferred writable members left read-only by rule: d3NetManager.timeout (fo-31, the one sensible failover knob), normal_preset (fo-32), state.lockedToDirector (fo-42). Promote timeout to a setValue control (e.g. 0 = disable auto failover) once the subsystem path and the units (seconds assumed) are verified.

### connection_templates

**Drafter summary.** Module-native part of the catalog: category 01 Connection (setup text + one connectionState indicator built on the module's own connection_status variable, no LiveUpdate subscription) and category 11 Templates (setup text + 11 generic rows with literal <OBJECT_PATH> / object.<PROPERTY_PATH> placeholders and the shared variable name myValue: three watch readouts (String, Number toFixed 2, JSON field x via jsonparse+jsonpath), five setters (Number 0, String, Boolean true, Boolean false, JSON partial {"x": 0.0}), two nudges (+1 / -1 with rotary companions) and one proposed Toggle row that falls back to the true/false pair). All strings were taken from src/feedbacks.ts, src/actions.ts, src/presets.ts (expression style), docs/INVENTORY.md and docs/PRESET_CATALOG_RULES.md; no candidate JSON path is used in this dimension. Text encoding convention: rows with textExpression=true carry a backtick template literal in which the newline is the two-character escape \n (exactly as src/presets.ts does today); rows with textExpression=false carry a real newline. All template rows share the pair (<OBJECT_PATH>, object.<PROPERTY_PATH>) and therefore the same variable name and frequency class (state, 500 ms).

**Verifier summary.** The candidate JSON (docs/research/phase0-candidates.json) has no connection/templates dimension (keys: monitoring, transport, track_layer, stage, exprvars, renderstream, failover), so every row correctly cites only "module:connectionState" or "template"; all strings were checked against src/feedbacks.ts, src/actions.ts, src/index.ts, src/variables.ts, src/presets.ts, docs/INVENTORY.md and docs/PRESET_CATALOG_RULES.md. Ids (conn_, tpl_) are unique and prefixed correctly, names are <= 40 chars, the 11 template rows share one pair (<OBJECT_PATH>, object.<PROPERTY_PATH>) with one variable name (myValue) and one freqClass (state), action option ids are exactly variableName/value (toggle takes variableName only, matching extension 4), Boolean values are real booleans, Number values are valid JS expressions, the JSON value parses, every textTemplate uses only existing expression functions (toFixed, jsonparse, jsonpath) and references $(liveupdate:myValue) of its own row, previewText and liveTestPriority are present on every row, no experimental rows exist and no candidate of status unverified/reject is used. Concrete defects found: (1) tpl_set_number.range and the tpl_setup text claim Infinity is rejected, but processNumberValue only checks typeof number && !isNaN, so Infinity passes (src/actions.ts:60-63); (2) previewText of tpl_watch_json / tpl_set_json shows 0.50 but jsonpath returns the raw number 0.5, so the button renders "0.5"; (3) conn_status understates the connectionState hazard: ws 'close' never calls checkFeedbacks() and no Director message arrives while disconnected, so the button stays GREEN with the text "Disconnected" for the whole outage, and after a connect it stays red until a subscriptions/valuesChanged message, which never comes on a page without any liveUpdateVariable feedback (src/index.ts:399-421, 556, 654); (4) the placeholder-guard description in tpl_setup omits the "$NA" and unparsed "$(" conditions from RULES §4 and the "PATH_ERROR (unsubscribed)" 3-strike variant; (5) nudge rows must state that the generator sets options.rotaryActions: true, otherwise rotate_left/rotate_right sets are inert; (6) the "wrap=false" jsonpath claim in tpl_watch_json notes is not verifiable from the local sources (the companion_api research text only confirms the function exists) and should be softened. Advisory for the lead (not a row failure): INVENTORY §5 records that the module never parses $(custom:...) in path options while RULES §4 relies on host-side parsing since module API 1.13; conn_setup repeats the RULES claim, which is fine if the lead's rule stands but should be confirmed in Phase 3. The draft's use of "object.<PROPERTY_PATH>" (RULES §4 says "<PROPERTY_PATH>") is acceptable because every property path must start with "object." and the literal placeholder is preserved. No missing rows: the module exposes nothing else natively; an optional "Watch: seconds as timecode" template would be a convenience, not a coverage gap.

<details><summary>Skipped candidates</summary>

- `module:liveUpdateVariable.defaults (track:track_1 / object.description / my_property / 0 ms)`: The feedback's built-in option defaults are legacy sanitised names with an unthrottled 0 ms frequency; the templates supersede them with explicit placeholders, the shared name myValue and the State interval. Not a row.
- `upstream-preset:trackLengthMonitor, screenNameMonitor, fpsMonitor, playheadMonitor, screenOffsetMonitor, screenOffsetSetPlus, screenOffsetSetMinus`: The seven presets in src/presets.ts belong to dimensions 02, 04, 05 and 07 (their paths are covered by candidates there); they also stack connectionState on readouts and use 0 ms frequencies, which the design rules forbid, so they are not carried into 01 Connection or 11 Templates.
- `module:connection_status (plain text without connectionState)`: A second connection row showing only the text would duplicate conn_status; the single indicator already carries the text and the boolean colour.

</details>

**Open questions:**

- conn_status colour can lag the real socket state: connectionState is only re-evaluated inside checkFeedbacks() on subscriptions/valuesChanged messages (INVENTORY §5). Is a one-line extension (call checkFeedbacks('connectionState') from the ws open/close handlers) acceptable alongside the approved extensions, so the indicator flips immediately?
- tpl_toggle depends on the proposed setToDisguiseToggle action (extension 4). If the extension is rejected, drop the row and keep tpl_set_true / tpl_set_false (listed in fallbackIds).
- Text encoding convention to confirm for the generator: textExpression=true rows carry a backtick template literal in which the newline is the two-character escape \n (matching src/presets.ts); textExpression=false rows carry a real newline character.
- tpl_watch_json / tpl_set_json use jsonpath(jsonparse(...), '$.x'); Companion 5 also supports member access on jsonparse results, so the generator may simplify to jsonparse($(liveupdate:myValue)).x if preferred.
- The placeholder guard (extension 1) must treat '<OBJECT_PATH>' and 'object.<PROPERTY_PATH>' as unset so unedited templates never reach the Director; confirm it also suppresses the per-evaluation warn log so a page of untouched templates does not spam the log.
- Templates share variableName myValue across all 11 rows by design; the HELP should warn that placing two edited templates for different paths requires renaming the variable in feedback, text and actions of each button.

## Cross-category critic (pre-merge)

Cross-category review of 8 dimension drafts (254 normal rows, 127 experimental rows, 16 text presets). Structural findings: one preset-id collision (conn_setup emitted by both failover and connection_templates), one hard variable-name conflict on a shared pair (Machine:"$(custom:d3_machine)" / object.hasTakenOverFailedMachine() is machineTakenOver in 10 Failover and machineHasTakenOver in 09 RenderStream), one custom-variable semantic overload (d3_layer means a timeline layer in 05/06 but an ExpressionVariables layer in ev_layer_vars), and one case-only near-collision (trackname vs trackName). The operator must create 21 d3_* custom variables (13 for normal-tier rows, 8 more for experimental rows); the conn_setup text lists only 11 of them and the fo/rs/monr setup texts describe d3_machine with three different definitions. OSC parity: trackname, volume, brightness and playMode ship at normal tier; currentSectionName, nextSectionName, sectionElapsed, sectionRemaining, bpm, trackposition and timecodeposition exist only as experimental (inferred) rows, and the two section-time rows report BEATS while the OSC module reports seconds. Essentials still missing after the verifiers' missingRows: current-track length (tr-22), UID-path renderLayer/isInError for LED walls, projector isInActiveStage, a normal-tier playhead-in-seconds (none doc-verified, so only a live-test priority), any control in 08 Expression Variables, and the disputed fo-18 NIC adaptor readout. Rule gaps the verifiers missed: doc-verified comprehensions placed in 99 Experimental keep status doc-verified (rule table has no such cell), 99 Experimental heading text presets are inconsistent across dimensions (ids monl_exp_setup/monr_exp_setup/trk_x_setup/stg_experimental_setup/ev_x_setup, none with homeCategory, and transport/renderstream/failover/connection ship none), and several rows use freqClass playhead on non-transport values without a visible freqRationale. After applying the per-dimension verifier removals (6 stage offset Y/Z rows, ev_uid_knob) and merging the duplicate takeover row, the catalog is 246 normal + 127 experimental rows.

### OSC parity

| disguise-osc variable | LiveUpdate preset | tier | note |
|---|---|---|---|
| `trackname` | `tr_trackname` | normal |  |
| `currentSectionName` | `tr_current_section_name` | normal |  |
| `nextSectionName` | `tr_next_section_name` | normal |  |
| `sectionElapsed` | `tr_section_elapsed` | normal | value in beats, the OSC module reports seconds |
| `sectionRemaining` | `tr_section_remaining` | normal | value in beats, the OSC module reports seconds |
| `volume` | `tr_volume` | normal |  |
| `brightness` | `tr_brightness` | normal |  |
| `bpm` | `tr_bpm` | normal |  |
| `playMode` | `tr_playmode` | normal |  |
| `trackposition` | `tr_trackposition` | normal |  |
| `timecodeposition` | `tr_timecodeposition` | normal |  |

### Variable-name conflicts (pre-merge)

- HARD CONFLICT (same pair, two names): objectPath Machine:"$(custom:d3_machine)" / propertyPath object.hasTakenOverFailedMachine() is variableName machineTakenOver (fo_taken_over, 10 Failover & d3Net, state) and machineHasTakenOver (rs_machine_takeover, 09 RenderStream, state). Rule 'exactly one variable name per pair' is broken; the second placed button would go stale. Resolution: delete rs_machine_takeover (rule: skip duplicates) and reference fo_taken_over from rs_setup, or keep it with variableName machineTakenOver and mark it crossRefOf fo_taken_over.
- CASE-ONLY NEAR-COLLISION: trackname (tr_trackname, transportManager:default / object.track.description, state) vs trackName (trk_track_name, track:"$(custom:d3_track)" / object.description, static). Different pairs so the rule is satisfied, but Companion variable ids differing only by case are a support hazard and tr's name is fixed by OSC parity. Rename trk_track_name's variable to selectedTrackName (and idxLayerName/layerName stay as they are).
- CUSTOM-VARIABLE SEMANTIC OVERLOAD: d3_layer is 'layer name inside d3_track' for all 05/06 rows but ev_layer_vars (module:"$(custom:d3_layer)") requires it to be an ExpressionVariables layer currently under the playhead. An operator who sets d3_layer = 'Video 1' for 06 Layer Control gets a PATH_ERROR (and 3-strike unsubscribe) on the EV row. Rename the EV row's variable to d3_evlayer.
- CUSTOM-VARIABLE DEFINITION DRIFT for d3_machine: fo_setup = 'resource name as in d3Net Manager / name field of GET /api/session/status/health'; monr_setup = 'Machine resource name as shown in Designer'; rs_setup = 'render node / machine name exactly as shown in the Cluster Workload widget Instances > Machine column' (used as the string argument of getStreamReceiveStatuses). These may or may not be the same string on r34.0.3; the catalog must pick one definition and rs_stream_receive must be live-tested against it.
- freqClass consistency is otherwise clean: every shared pair (brightness, volume, engaged, layerEnabled, layerTStart, layerTLength, keyPosX, keyPosY, keyScale, keyBrightness, brightnessConstant, idxKeyPosX, trackTcAdjust, screenOffset, screenOffsetX, screenMasterFade, screenHoldOutput, screenRenderLayer, screenExternalUpdate, projectorMasterFade, projectorHoldOutput, projectorRenderMode, projectorShutterOpen, projectorDynBlend, projectorFeather, uidMasterFade, uidHoldOutput, evFloat, myValue) carries one name and one class in all rows that use it.
- Naming-convention inconsistency (not a rule breach): the same concept is named sectionCount (tr), trackNSections (trk); bpm (tr, OSC), trackBpm (trk); machineNodeName (monr, object.nodeName), machineNodeId (fo, object.nodeId), machineName (fo, object.name), remoteNodeName (monr, monitor object.nodeName). Acceptable, but document them side by side in the catalog README.

### Missing essentials (pre-merge)

- tr_track_length_beats (tr-22, doc-verified): transportManager:default / object.track.lengthInBeats, variableName currentTrackLengthBeats, static - the denominator for a 'playhead / length' display; without it the only length readout is for the d3_track selection, not the track that is actually playing (already in transport missingRows; confirmed as a real gap).
- Playhead in seconds / mm:ss at normal tier: tr_trackposition and tr_timecodeposition are experimental because beatToTime/beatToTimecode combinations are inferred. No doc-verified path exists, so this cannot be fixed by drafting - make tr_trackposition, tr_current_section_name and tr_section_remaining the top three live-test items, since these are the OSC-module variables operators migrate from.
- LED/DMX wall 'off stage' and health via the UID route: stg_uid_render_layer + stg_uid_on_stage + stg_uid_off_stage (stage-009 + stage-055) and stg_uid_in_error (stage-021 + stage-055). The UID path is the only normal-tier route to an LED wall and today it carries only master_fade and holdOutput (in stage missingRows; confirmed).
- stg_proj_active_stage: projector:"$(custom:d3_projector)" / object.isInActiveStage() (stage-020 + stage-034) - a projector in an inactive stage outputs nothing and no current row reveals it (in stage missingRows; confirmed).
- 08 Expression Variables has no control at all after ev_uid_knob is removed; ev-device-by-uid is doc-verified writable=yes, so ev_uid_float_up / ev_uid_float_down nudges on evFloat are permitted by the tier rules - lead ruling needed on the list-subscript set caveat (in exprvars missingRows; confirmed). Also ev_uid_description (getByUID($(custom:d3_evuid)) / object.description) as an index-independent smoke test.
- Layer opacity: trk_key_opacity + lay_opacity_plus/minus/full on object.findSequence("opacity").sequence.key(0).v - same footing as scale/pos.y which already ship (in track_layer missingRows; confirmed).
- fo_network_adaptors (fo-18, doc-verified comprehension): NIC link up/down and link speed per machine - a downed 10G link is a classic pre-show fault; blocked only by the 'no comprehension in normal tier' rule, so needs the same lead ruling as the trk_layer_names_x / trk_key_posx_all_x rows.
- Stage master-fade half-point stg_screen_fade_half (0.5) - optional, stage-007 notes suggest it.
- Not a row, but a catalog-level gap operators will ask about first: no play / stop / next-section / go-to controls exist anywhere (LiveUpdate cannot issue TransportCommands). tr_setup says so; conn_setup and the README should say it in the first paragraph, and point at the REST /api/session/transport/* endpoints so nobody hunts for a missing preset.

### Rule violations (pre-merge)

- Tier rule has no cell for 'doc-verified candidate whose expression is a comprehension': trk_layer_names_x and trk_key_posx_all_x sit in 99 Experimental with status doc-verified, while fo-18 (same situation) was dropped entirely and stage's stage-028/044 comprehensions were demoted to unverified. Three dimensions resolved the same gap three ways; the lead must add a rule (suggested: experimental tier, catalog status 'doc-verified (comprehension)', read-only) and apply it uniformly.
- 99 Experimental heading text presets are inconsistent: monitoring emits monl_exp_setup + monr_exp_setup, track_layer trk_x_setup, stage stg_experimental_setup, exprvars ev_x_setup; transport, renderstream, failover and connection emit none. None of the five carries homeCategory, so the generator cannot place them under the right heading. Either the generator synthesises the headings from homeCategory (then delete all five) or every dimension ships exactly one <prefix>_exp_setup with homeCategory set.
- rs_machine_takeover duplicates fo_taken_over on the same pair with a different variableName (see variableConflicts) - breaks the one-name-per-pair rule at catalog level; the renderstream verifier flagged it but the failover side was never reconciled.
- conn_setup duplicate id (see idCollisions) - breaks preset-id uniqueness and the 'each category starts with <prefix>_setup' rule can only be satisfied once.
- Experimental id conventions differ per dimension: track_layer suffix _x (trk_section_table_x), stage infix _x_ (stg_x_led_name), renderstream infix _exp_ (rs_exp_min_fps), monitoring/transport/failover/exprvars/connection no marker (monr_gpu_total, tr_bpm, fo_solo_mode, ev_dev_float, conn_in_show). Ids are 'stable' once shipped, so decide now (recommend: no marker in the id; the generator already prefixes names with '[EXP] ').
- freqClass deviations without visible freqRationale: stg_screen_offset / stg_screen_offset_reset / stg_screen_offset_x(+up/down) / stg_screen_rotation / stg_x_led_offset use playhead (250 ms) on vectors (class table: state), tr_trackname uses state on a name (table: static), tr_preroll and tr_tc_incoming use playhead (defensible), ev_layer_vars uses playhead on a comprehension over module fields (expensive at 250 ms), fo_health_worst / fo_health_states / fo_offline_machines / fo_failed_machines / fo_takenover_machines / fo_machines_table / fo_understudy_table use monitoring on comprehensions over every machine. The compact rows do not show freqRationale, so the merge step must reject any deviating row that lacks one.
- rs_inst_machine textTemplate embeds $(custom:d3_instance) in button text (renderstream verifier finding, confirmed against phase0-candidates.json:6201 - Companion rewrites every $(label:var) except $(local:...) to the connection label on preset import). Cross-category consequence: the generator needs a hard check that preset text contains only $(liveupdate:<variableName>) references, because ev_uid_knob (references $(liveupdate:evName) which is not its own variable) and trk_section_start-style rows are likely to repeat the pattern.
- Name length after the '[EXP] ' prefix: conn_setup says experimental presets are renamed '[EXP] ...' (6 chars). 'EV device description (smoke test)' (34) becomes exactly 40, 'EV all variables (detailed list)' 38, 'Monitoring Remote (experimental)' 38. All pass but with no margin; the generator must enforce the 40-char limit on the post-prefix name.
- ev_uid_knob (exprvars verifier finding, confirmed): identical pair + variableName + freqClass to ev_uid_float - a duplicate row, and its id promises a control that does not exist.
- stage y/z offset rows (stg_screen_offset_y/_z and their 4 nudges) reference members (object.offset.y / .z) absent from the candidate JSON (stage verifier finding, confirmed) - must be dropped and stg_setup text corrected; stg_screen_offset_x_up/down gated on a live scalar-set test.
- trk_cue_count is named 'Cue count' but subscribes object.cueBeats() (a list) under variableName trackCueBeats; with the jsonparse(...).length template already rejected by the verifier, either rename the preset 'Cue beats (list)' or accept jsonpath('$.length') and keep the name.
- 01 Connection conn_status carries candidateId 'module:connectionState' and empty object/property paths - not a candidate-JSON member; acceptable only if the generator special-cases it (connectionState feedback, no liveUpdateVariable feedback, no Update Frequency). Flag so the merge validator does not reject it.

### Recommendations

- Merge step, blocking: (1) drop the failover-authored conn_setup and move its six experimental conn_* ids into the connection_templates conn_setup text; (2) delete rs_machine_takeover or rename its variable to machineTakenOver with a crossRef to fo_taken_over; (3) rename ev_layer_vars' custom variable to d3_evlayer; (4) apply the per-dimension verifier removals (ev_uid_knob, six stage Y/Z offset rows) and gate stg_screen_offset_x_up/down behind the live scalar-set test; (5) add tr_track_length_beats (tr-22) as currentTrackLengthBeats.
- Unify the d3_machine definition in fo_setup, monr_setup, rs_setup and conn_setup to one sentence (Machine resource name as shown in d3Net Manager / 'name' of GET /api/session/status/health), and add 'live-test rs_stream_receive with that name' to the RS open questions.
- Rewrite conn_setup as the single source of truth for custom variables: list all 14 normal-tier variables (add d3_layerindex, d3_section, d3_beat, d3_screenuid) with type (quoted name vs unquoted integer/0x literal), then the 7 experimental-only ones under a separate heading. Per-category setup texts should only repeat the variables they use.
- Add a rule row for doc-verified comprehensions (experimental tier, read-only, status 'doc-verified (comprehension)') and re-apply it to trk_layer_names_x, trk_key_posx_all_x, stage-028/044 and the dropped fo-18 network adaptors row so all four dimensions treat the case the same way.
- Let the generator synthesise 99 Experimental headings from homeCategory and delete the five hand-written heading presets (or, if the lead prefers hand-written text, require exactly one <prefix>_exp_setup per home category with homeCategory set, including new ones for 04, 09, 10 and 01).
- Generator validation checklist to add before Phase 2: preset id unique; variableName unique per pair and pair unique per name across the whole catalog; button text may reference only $(liveupdate:<own variableName>); no $(custom:...) in text or variableName; name length <= 40 after the '[EXP] ' prefix; freqRationale mandatory when freqClass differs from the class table; experimental rows have zero actions; every toggle row lists existing onOff fallback ids (currently satisfied: tr_engage/tr_disengage, lay_enable_on/off, stg_screen_hold_on/off, stg_proj_hold_on/off, stg_uid_hold_on/off, tpl_set_true/false).
- Rename trk_track_name's variable from trackName to selectedTrackName to avoid the case-only clash with the OSC-parity variable trackname.
- Live-test priority list (in order): tr_current_section_name / tr_next_section_name / tr_section_remaining / tr_trackposition (OSC migration path), trk_layer_name_idx and trk_key_posx_idx (doc-verbatim reference pairs), ev_uid_float set through the list subscript, stg_screen_offset_x_up scalar set, rs_stream_receive machine-name argument, monr_fps with and without MonitoringManager.useMonitorByName.
- Document the beats-vs-seconds difference for sectionElapsed / sectionRemaining next to the OSC parity table so operators migrating from companion-module-disguise-osc do not read beats as seconds; if a seconds variant is ever verified, give it a distinct name (sectionRemainingSec) rather than changing units under the OSC name.
- State in the catalog README's first paragraph that transport commands (play/stop/section navigation/go-to) are not reachable over LiveUpdate and point to the REST endpoints, so the absence of those presets is understood as a protocol limit rather than a catalog omission.

## Module extensions required by the catalog

See [PRESET_CATALOG_RULES.md §11](PRESET_CATALOG_RULES.md). Rows with `stateColour` use the `liveUpdateCompare` feedback; rows of kind `toggle` use the `setToDisguiseToggle` action and list their on/off siblings.
